建议：叉积是「造法向量」的万能钥匙，平面的方程全靠它。前三站打牢，直线和平面只是把向量「组装」起来。Tip: the cross product is the master key for making normal vectors — the whole plane section rests on it. Master the first three stops and lines/planes are just vectors bolted together.

1 三维向量回顾3D vectors: a quick review 3D vector review三维向量回顾
这一站要你会：用 i、j、k 表示三维向量，会算加减、数乘、模长，会求单位向量。Goal: write 3D vectors in i, j, k form, and add, scale, take the magnitude and find the unit vector.
平面向量你早熟了，三维只是多一条 z 轴。一个三维向量 a = a₁i + a₂j + a₃k，说白了就是「沿 x 轴走 a₁、沿 y 轴走 a₂、沿 z 轴走 a₃」这三段路程拼成的位移。所以加减就是对应分量相加减，数乘就是每个分量都乘同一个数，模长就是三个分量平方和再开根——把二维的毕达哥拉斯「加高一层」。单位向量则是「只留方向、丢掉长度」：除以模长，长度正好变成 1。You already know plane vectors; 3D just adds a z-axis. A 3D vector a = a₁i + a₂j + a₃k is simply a displacement built from three legs: a₁ along x, a₂ along y, a₃ along z. So add/subtract is component-wise, scalar multiply is multiply every component, and the magnitude is the square root of the sum of squares — Pythagoras with one more floor. The unit vector is "keep the direction, drop the length": divide by the magnitude and the length becomes exactly 1.
三维向量三件套：The three tools for a 3D vector:
a = a₁i + a₂j + a₃k |a| = √(a₁² + a₂² + a₃²) â = a / |a|
  • 表示：a = a₁i + a₂j + a₃k = (a₁, a₂, a₃)。Form: a = a₁i + a₂j + a₃k = (a₁, a₂, a₃).
  • 模长：|a| = √(a₁² + a₂² + a₃²)。Magnitude: |a| = √(a₁² + a₂² + a₃²).
  • 单位向量：â = a / |a|，方向不变、长度变 1。Unit vector: â = a / |a|, same direction, length 1.
单位向量用在哪儿：后面求法向量、求方向向量，常常只要方向、不要长度，就先单位化。看到「unit vector」这个词，反应就一条：除以模长。Where unit vectors appear: later, normal and direction vectors often need only direction, not length, so you normalise first. The reflex for the words "unit vector" is one move: divide by the magnitude.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: A vector in three dimensions is written a = a₁i + a₂j + a₃k. Its magnitude is |a| = √(a₁² + a₂² + a₃²), and the unit vector in the direction of a is a / |a|.
例题Example
已知 a = 3i + 4j + 12k，求它的模长 |a| 和单位向量 â。Given a = 3i + 4j + 12k, find its magnitude |a| and the unit vector â.
① 模长：|a| = √(3² + 4² + 12²) = √(9 + 16 + 144) = √169 = 13。① magnitude: |a| = √(3² + 4² + 12²) = √(9 + 16 + 144) = √169 = 13. ② 单位向量：â = a/13 = (3/13)i + (4/13)j + (12/13)k。② unit vector: â = a/13 = (3/13)i + (4/13)j + (12/13)k.
练习Practice
先算三个分量的平方和再开根；单位向量就是把每个分量都除以模长。Square the three components, add, then root; the unit vector divides every component by the magnitude.

已知 a = 2i − 2j + k，求 |a| 和单位向量 â。Given a = 2i − 2j + k, find |a| and the unit vector â.

点开看答案Show answer
|a| = √(2² + (−2)² + 1²) = √(4 + 4 + 1) = √9 = 3。
â = (2/3)i − (2/3)j + (1/3)k。 |a| = √(2² + (−2)² + 1²) = √(4 + 4 + 1) = √9 = 3.
â = (2/3)i − (2/3)j + (1/3)k.
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 点积与夹角The dot product and angles Dot product点积
这一站要你会：用分量式算点积，用 cosθ = a·b/(|a||b|) 求夹角，记住垂直 ⟺ a·b = 0。Goal: compute dot products from components, find angles via cosθ = a·b/(|a||b|), and recall that perpendicular ⇔ a·b = 0.
点积量的是「两个向量有多合拍」：方向越一致，点积越大；完全垂直，点积正好是 0。它有两条等价的公式——分量式 a·b = a₁b₁ + a₂b₂ + a₃b₃ 负责「算」，几何式 a·b = |a||b| cosθ 负责「解释夹角」。把两条连起来，就能从分量直接反推出夹角。最常用的一句：垂直 ⟺ 点积为零，看到垂直先写 a·b = 0。The dot product measures how aligned two vectors are: the closer the directions, the bigger the value; if they're exactly perpendicular, it is zero. It has two equivalent forms — the component form a·b = a₁b₁ + a₂b₂ + a₃b₃ does the arithmetic, the geometric form a·b = |a||b| cosθ explains the angle. Chain the two and you can recover the angle straight from components. The line used most: perpendicular ⇔ dot product zero — the moment you see "perpendicular", write a·b = 0.
点积两条公式 + 一个判定：Two dot-product formulas + one test:
a·b = a₁b₁ + a₂b₂ + a₃b₃ a·b = |a||b| cos θ cos θ = a·b / (|a||b|)
  • 垂直判定：a ⊥ b ⟺ a·b = 0。Perpendicular: a ⊥ b ⟺ a·b = 0.
  • 投影：a 在 b 上的投影长度 = (a·b) / |b|。Projection: the length of a projected onto b is (a·b) / |b|.
别把点积和叉积搞混：点积的结果是一个数（标量），叉积的结果是一个向量。点积为零是「垂直」，叉积为零是「平行或反向」。Don't mix up dot and cross: the dot product gives a number (a scalar), the cross product gives a vector. Zero dot product means perpendicular; zero cross product means parallel or opposite.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The dot product of a and b is a·b = a₁b₁ + a₂b₂ + a₃b₃ = |a||b| cos θ. Two vectors are perpendicular if and only if a·b = 0.
例题Example
a = (1, 2, 3)，b = (4, 5, 6)。求 a·b，并说明这两个向量接近平行还是接近垂直。For a = (1, 2, 3) and b = (4, 5, 6), find a·b and say whether they are closer to parallel or to perpendicular.
① 点积：a·b = 1×4 + 2×5 + 3×6 = 4 + 10 + 18 = 32。① dot product: a·b = 1×4 + 2×5 + 3×6 = 4 + 10 + 18 = 32. ② |a| = √14，|b| = √77，cosθ = 32/(√14·√77) = 32/√1078 ≈ 0.97 → θ ≈ 13°。② |a| = √14, |b| = √77, so cosθ = 32/(√14·√77) = 32/√1078 ≈ 0.97 → θ ≈ 13°. ③ 夹角很小，所以接近平行。③ the angle is tiny, so they are close to parallel.
练习Practice
算点积，看是不是 0。Work out the dot product and see if it is 0.

判断 a = (2, 2, 1) 与 b = (1, −2, 2) 是否垂直。Decide whether a = (2, 2, 1) and b = (1, −2, 2) are perpendicular.

点开看答案Show answer
a·b = 2×1 + 2×(−2) + 1×2 = 2 − 4 + 2 = 0，所以两向量垂直。 a·b = 2×1 + 2×(−2) + 1×2 = 2 − 4 + 2 = 0, so the vectors are perpendicular.
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 叉积The cross product Cross product叉积
这一站要你会：用分量公式算 a×b，知道结果垂直于 a、b，知道 |a×b| 是平行四边形面积，会用右手定则判断方向。Goal: compute a×b from components, know it is perpendicular to a and b, know |a×b| is the parallelogram area, and use the right-hand rule for its direction.
叉积是进阶数学的「造向量机器」：喂进去两个向量 a 和 b，吐出来一个新的向量，它同时垂直于 a 和 b，长度等于 a、b 张成的平行四边形面积。方向用右手定则定：右手四指从 a 弯向 b，大拇指指的方向就是 a×b。注意叉积只在三维里存在，而且反交换——a×b 和 b×a 正好相反。后面求平面法向量，靠的就是它。The cross product is Further Maths's "vector factory": feed in a and b, and out comes a new vector that is perpendicular to both, with length equal to the area of the parallelogram they span. The direction follows the right-hand rule: curl your right-hand fingers from a toward b, and your thumb points along a×b. Note the cross product only exists in 3D, and it is anti-commutative — a×b and b×a point opposite ways. This is exactly how you'll make normal vectors for planes.
叉积分量公式 + 几何意义：Cross product in components + its meaning:
a×b = (a₂b₃ − a₃b₂)i + (a₃b₁ − a₁b₃)j + (a₁b₂ − a₂b₁)k |a×b| = |a||b| sin θ = 平行四边形面积
  • 垂直：a×b ⊥ a 且 a×b ⊥ b。Perpendicular: a×b ⊥ a and a×b ⊥ b.
  • 反交换：a×b = −(b×a)。Anti-commutative: a×b = −(b×a).
  • 方向：右手定则。Direction: the right-hand rule.
记分量公式有口诀：「交叉相乘再相减」。第一个分量用 a、b 的第 2、3 分量交叉；第二个用第 3、1；第三个用第 1、2。写多了就顺了。A mnemonic for the components: "cross-multiply then subtract". The first component crosses the 2nd and 3rd components, the second crosses the 3rd and 1st, the third crosses the 1st and 2nd.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The cross product a×b is a vector perpendicular to both a and b. Its magnitude |a×b| = |a||b| sin θ equals the area of the parallelogram spanned by a and b, and its direction follows the right-hand rule.
例题Example
a = (1, 2, 3)，b = (4, 5, 6)。求 a×b，并验证它垂直于 a 和 b。For a = (1, 2, 3) and b = (4, 5, 6), find a×b and verify it is perpendicular to both.
① 叉积：a×b = (2·6 − 3·5, 3·4 − 1·6, 1·5 − 2·4) = (12 − 15, 12 − 6, 5 − 8) = (−3, 6, −3)。① cross product: a×b = (2·6 − 3·5, 3·4 − 1·6, 1·5 − 2·4) = (12 − 15, 12 − 6, 5 − 8) = (−3, 6, −3). ② 验证垂直：a·(a×b) = 1×(−3) + 2×6 + 3×(−3) = −3 + 12 − 9 = 0 ✓；b·(a×b) = 4×(−3) + 5×6 + 6×(−3) = −12 + 30 − 18 = 0 ✓。② check perpendicular: a·(a×b) = 1×(−3) + 2×6 + 3×(−3) = −3 + 12 − 9 = 0 ✓; b·(a×b) = 4×(−3) + 5×6 + 6×(−3) = −12 + 30 − 18 = 0 ✓.
练习Practice
代入分量公式，方向用右手定则判断。Use the component formula, then get the direction from the right-hand rule.

a = (2, 0, 0)，b = (0, 3, 0)。求 a×b，并说明它的方向。For a = (2, 0, 0) and b = (0, 3, 0), find a×b and describe its direction.

点开看答案Show answer
a×b = (0·0 − 0·3, 0·0 − 2·0, 2·3 − 0·0) = (0, 0, 6)，指向 z 轴正方向（右手四指从 +x 扫向 +y，拇指朝 +z）。模长 6 = 矩形面积 2×3 ✓。 a×b = (0·0 − 0·3, 0·0 − 2·0, 2·3 − 0·0) = (0, 0, 6), pointing along the positive z-axis (fingers from +x to +y, thumb along +z). Length 6 = the 2×3 rectangle's area ✓.
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 直线的方程Equations of lines Line equations直线方程
这一站要你会：写直线的向量式 r = a + tb，转成对称式，并判断两直线相交、平行还是异面。Goal: write a line as r = a + tb, convert to symmetric form, and decide whether two lines intersect, are parallel, or are skew.
空间里的直线，只要两个信息就齐了：一个点 a（起点在哪）和一个方向 b（往哪走）。写成 r = a + tb，t 取遍所有实数——t 就是「沿方向 b 走了几步」，走正走负、走多走少，就扫出整条直线。把 x、y、z 三个分量分别写出来，再消掉 t，就得到对称式 (x−x₀)/l = (y−y₀)/m = (z−z₀)/n。两条直线的关系只有三种：相交（有公共点）、平行（方向成比例）、异面（既不平行也不相交）。A line in space needs just two facts: a point a (where it sits) and a direction b (where it heads). Write r = a + tb with t running over all reals — t is simply "how many steps along b", and as t varies you sweep out the whole line. Write the x, y, z components separately and eliminate t to get the symmetric form (x−x₀)/l = (y−y₀)/m = (z−z₀)/n. Two lines have only three possible relationships: intersecting (a shared point), parallel (proportional directions), or skew (neither).
直线两种写法 + 三种关系：Two forms of a line + three relationships:
r = a + t b, t ∈ ℝ (x − x₀)/l = (y − y₀)/m = (z − z₀)/n
  • 向量式 r = a + tb：a 是位置向量，b 是方向向量。Vector form r = a + tb: a is a position vector, b the direction vector.
  • 对称式由向量式消去 t 得到。Symmetric form comes from eliminating t.
  • 平行 ⟺ 方向向量成比例；异面 ⟺ 不平行且无公共点。Parallel ⇔ proportional directions; skew ⇔ neither parallel nor sharing a point.
判断相交还是异面的实操：把两直线都写成参数式，令 x、y、z 三组分量分别相等，解 t 和 s。三个方程两个未知数，有解就相交、无解就异面。Intersect or skew, in practice: write both lines parametrically, set the x, y and z components equal, and solve for t and s. Three equations in two unknowns — solvable means intersecting, not solvable means skew.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: A line through the point with position vector a and direction b has equation r = a + tb, where t is a real parameter. Equating components and eliminating t gives the symmetric form (x−x₀)/l = (y−y₀)/m = (z−z₀)/n.
例题Example
直线过点 (1, 2, 3)，方向向量 b = (2, −1, 4)。写出它的向量式和对称式。A line passes through (1, 2, 3) with direction b = (2, −1, 4). Write its vector and symmetric equations.
① 向量式：r = (1, 2, 3) + t(2, −1, 4)。① vector form: r = (1, 2, 3) + t(2, −1, 4). ② 对称式：(x−1)/2 = (y−2)/(−1) = (z−3)/4，即 (x−1)/2 = −(y−2) = (z−3)/4。② symmetric form: (x−1)/2 = (y−2)/(−1) = (z−3)/4, i.e. (x−1)/2 = −(y−2) = (z−3)/4.
练习Practice
直接套 (x−x₀)/l = (y−y₀)/m = (z−z₀)/n。Apply (x−x₀)/l = (y−y₀)/m = (z−z₀)/n directly.

直线过点 (0, 1, −2)，方向向量 b = (3, 2, −1)。写出对称式。A line passes through (0, 1, −2) with direction b = (3, 2, −1). Write the symmetric equation.

点开看答案Show answer
x/3 = (y−1)/2 = (z+2)/(−1)，即 x/3 = (y−1)/2 = −(z+2)。 x/3 = (y−1)/2 = (z+2)/(−1), i.e. x/3 = (y−1)/2 = −(z+2).
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 平面的方程Equations of planes Plane equations平面方程
这一站要你会：用 r·n = d 写平面方程，知道 n 是法向量，会求点到平面的距离，会求直线与平面的交点。Goal: write a plane as r·n = d, know n is the normal vector, find the distance from a point to a plane, and find where a line meets a plane.
平面不用「点 + 方向」，而用一个法向量 n 来定：r·n = d，意思是「平面上任意一点 r 在法向量 n 上的投影都等于同一个定值 d」。这就是点法式。给三个点求平面？先叉积出法向量，再代一个点求 d。求点到平面的距离有现成公式；求直线与平面的交点，就是把直线参数式代入平面方程解 t。A plane is fixed not by "point + direction" but by a normal vector n: r·n = d, meaning "every point r on the plane has the same projection d onto n". This is the normal form. Given three points, cross two direction vectors to get n, then substitute one point to find d. There's a ready formula for the distance from a point to a plane, and to find where a line meets a plane, substitute the line's parametric form into the plane equation and solve for t.
平面点法式 + 距离公式：Normal form of a plane + the distance formula:
r · n = d distance = |p·n − d| / |n|
  • 点法式 r·n = d：n 是法向量，d 是常数。Normal form r·n = d: n is the normal vector, d a constant.
  • 点到平面距离 = |p·n − d| / |n|。Distance from point to plane = |p·n − d| / |n|.
  • 线面相交：把 r = a + tb 代入 r·n = d 解 t。Line meets plane: substitute r = a + tb into r·n = d and solve for t.
法向量怎么来？已知平面上两个方向，叉积一下就是法向量。上一站学的叉积，正好在这里兑现。Where do normals come from? Given two directions in the plane, take their cross product — that's the normal. The cross product from the last stop pays off right here.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The plane with normal vector n has equation r·n = d, where d is a constant. The perpendicular distance from a point with position vector p to the plane r·n = d is |p·n − d| / |n|.
例题Example
平面过点 (1, 0, 0)，法向量 n = (2, 3, 4)。求平面方程。A plane passes through (1, 0, 0) with normal n = (2, 3, 4). Find its equation.
① 设 r = (x, y, z)，则 r·n = 2x + 3y + 4z。① with r = (x, y, z), r·n = 2x + 3y + 4z. ② 代入点 (1, 0, 0)：d = 2×1 + 3×0 + 4×0 = 2。② substitute (1, 0, 0): d = 2×1 + 3×0 + 4×0 = 2. ③ 平面方程：2x + 3y + 4z = 2。③ plane equation: 2x + 3y + 4z = 2.
练习Practice
套公式 |p·n − d| / |n|，先算 p·n。Use |p·n − d| / |n|; compute p·n first.

求点 (1, 1, 1) 到平面 2x + 3y + 4z = 2 的距离。Find the distance from (1, 1, 1) to the plane 2x + 3y + 4z = 2.

点开看答案Show answer
p·n = 2×1 + 3×1 + 4×1 = 9；|n| = √(4 + 9 + 16) = √29。
距离 = |9 − 2|/√29 = 7/√29。 p·n = 2×1 + 3×1 + 4×1 = 9; |n| = √(4 + 9 + 16) = √29.
distance = |9 − 2|/√29 = 7/√29.
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —

单元 6 小结Unit 6 summary

① 三维向量：a = a₁i + a₂j + a₃k，模长 √(a₁²+a₂²+a₃²)，单位向量 a/|a|。
② 点积 a·b = a₁b₁+a₂b₂+a₃b₃ = |a||b|cosθ；垂直 ⟺ a·b = 0。
③ 叉积 a×b 垂直于 a、b，|a×b| 是平行四边形面积，右手定则定方向。
④ 直线 r = a + tb，对称式 (x−x₀)/l = (y−y₀)/m = (z−z₀)/n。
⑤ 平面 r·n = d，n 是法向量；点到平面距离 = |p·n−d|/|n|。① 3D vectors: a = a₁i + a₂j + a₃k, magnitude √(a₁²+a₂²+a₃²), unit vector a/|a|.
② dot product a·b = a₁b₁+a₂b₂+a₃b₃ = |a||b|cosθ; perpendicular ⇔ a·b = 0.
③ cross product a×b is perpendicular to a and b, |a×b| is the parallelogram area, right-hand rule.
④ line r = a + tb, symmetric form (x−x₀)/l = (y−y₀)/m = (z−z₀)/n.
⑤ plane r·n = d with normal n; distance from a point to a plane = |p·n−d|/|n|.

  3. 证明整除性Induction for divisibility

建议：先在第 1 站把「基础情形 + 归纳假设 + 归纳步」三要素彻底搞懂，后面几站只是往同一个骨架里填不同的数学内容。Tip: nail the three parts — base case, hypothesis, induction step — in stop 1 first; the later stops just fill different maths into the same skeleton.

1 归纳法是什么：多米诺骨牌法The idea of induction The domino principle多米诺原理
这一站要你会：说清归纳法的三要素（基础情形、归纳假设、归纳步），并理解为什么它们合起来就「一劳永逸」证明了对所有 n 成立。Goal: state the three parts of induction (base case, induction hypothesis, induction step) and see why together they prove the statement for all n in one go.
数学归纳法就是推多米诺骨牌。你想证明「一排牌全都会倒」，不必一张一张去推，只要确认两件事：① 第一张真的倒了（这叫「基础情形」）；② 每一张倒了都一定能带倒下一张（这叫「归纳步」）。两件事一合，结论自动成立——第一张倒 → 第二张倒 → 第三张倒 → …… 一路推下去，无穷无尽。数学里也一样：要证「对所有 n，命题 P(n) 都成立」，只要证 P(1) 成立，再证「P(k) 成立能推出 P(k+1) 成立」，就一步到位了。Proof by induction is a row of dominoes. To show "the whole row falls" you never push each one by hand — you check two things: ① the first one really falls (the "base case"); ② each falling one knocks over the next (the "induction step"). Together they make the conclusion automatic — the first falls, then the second, then the third, on and on forever. In maths: to prove "P(n) holds for all n", prove P(1), then prove "P(k) true forces P(k+1) true".
  • 基础情形：验证 n=1（或最小起点）时命题成立。Base case: check the statement for n = 1 (or the smallest starting n).
  • 归纳假设：假设 n=k 时命题成立。Induction hypothesis: assume the statement holds for n = k.
  • 归纳步：由 n=k 成立，推出 n=k+1 也成立。Induction step: from n = k, deduce n = k+1.
  • 为什么够用：P(1) → P(2) → P(3) → …… 像多米诺一路倒下，覆盖所有正整数。Why it works: P(1) → P(2) → P(3) → … falls like dominoes, covering every positive integer.
起点不一定是 1：有时命题从 n=0 或 n=2 才开始成立，基础情形就验那个最小的 n。先看清题目要你证明的范围，别想当然从 1 开始。The start need not be 1: sometimes a statement only begins at n = 0 or n = 2 — check the smallest n in the given range. Read what you must prove; don't assume you start at 1.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: We prove by induction on n. First, the base case: ... . Now assume the statement holds for n = k; we show it holds for n = k+1. Hence, by the principle of mathematical induction, the statement is true for all n.
例题Example
用归纳法的语言，为「证明 2ⁿ ≥ 2n 对所有正整数 n 成立」写出前两步（基础情形 + 归纳假设），并说明第三步要证明什么。For "2ⁿ ≥ 2n for all positive integers n", write the first two parts (base case and induction hypothesis), and state what the third part must prove.
① 基础情形：n=1 时，左边 2¹=2，右边 2·1=2，2≥2 成立 ✓。① base case: at n=1, LHS 2¹=2, RHS 2·1=2, and 2≥2 ✓. ② 归纳假设：设 n=k 时成立，即 2ᵏ ≥ 2k。② hypothesis: assume true for n=k, i.e. 2ᵏ ≥ 2k. ③ 归纳步：要证明由它推出 2ᵏ⁺¹ ≥ 2(k+1)。（具体怎么推，下一站学技巧。）③ step: show this forces 2ᵏ⁺¹ ≥ 2(k+1). (The how comes in the next stops.)
练习Practice
三要素各说一句即可，不用真的算出结果。One sentence per part is enough — no need to finish the proof.

要为「证明 n! ≥ 2ⁿ⁻¹ 对所有正整数 n 成立」写归纳法框架，请写出：基础情形（验哪个 n？）、归纳假设、以及归纳步要证的目标。For "n! ≥ 2ⁿ⁻¹ for all positive integers n", write the base case (which n?), the induction hypothesis, and the target of the induction step.

点开看答案Show answer
基础情形：n=1，1! = 1 ≥ 2⁰ = 1 ✓。
归纳假设：设 k! ≥ 2ᵏ⁻¹。
归纳步：要证 (k+1)! ≥ 2ᵏ。 Base case: n=1, 1! = 1 ≥ 2⁰ = 1 ✓.
Hypothesis: assume k! ≥ 2ᵏ⁻¹.
Step: prove (k+1)! ≥ 2ᵏ.
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 证明级数求和公式Induction for series Proving Σr = n(n+1)/2证级数求和
这一站要你会：用归纳法完整证明 Σr = n(n+1)/2 这类求和公式，掌握「验证 → 假设 → 递推」三步的标准写法。Goal: prove a sum like Σr = n(n+1)/2 by induction, and master the standard "verify → assume → prove" layout.
级数求和是归纳法最经典的「练手题」。要证 1+2+3+…+n = n(n+1)/2 对一切 n 成立，第一步先验 n=1：左边 1、右边 1·2/2=1，相等 ✓。第二步假设 n=k 成立，也就是 1+2+…+k = k(k+1)/2。第三步最关键——要证 n=k+1 时也对，也就是 1+2+…+k+(k+1) = (k+1)(k+2)/2。窍门：左边的前 k 项正好就是归纳假设里的那串，直接把它换成 k(k+1)/2，再加最后一项 (k+1)，通分一算就出来。这招「把前面部分套用假设」，是归纳步的万能钥匙。Series sums are induction's classic warm-up. To prove 1+2+3+…+n = n(n+1)/2 for all n, first check n=1: left 1, right 1·2/2=1 — equal ✓. Then assume n=k, i.e. 1+2+…+k = k(k+1)/2. The third part matters most — show n=k+1 works, i.e. 1+2+…+k+(k+1) = (k+1)(k+2)/2. The trick: the first k terms on the left are exactly the hypothesis, so swap them for k(k+1)/2, add the last term (k+1), and simplify. This "apply the hypothesis to the front part" move is the master key of every induction step.
证明 Σr = n(n+1)/2 的三步：Three steps for Σr = n(n+1)/2:
1 + 2 + … + k = k(k+1)/2 1 + 2 + … + k + (k+1) = k(k+1)/2 + (k+1) = (k+1)(k/2 + 1) = (k+1)(k+2)/2 ✓
  • 基础：n=1，左 = 1，右 = 1·2/2 = 1 ✓。Base: n=1, left = 1, right = 1·2/2 = 1 ✓.
  • 假设：1+2+…+k = k(k+1)/2。Hypothesis: 1+2+…+k = k(k+1)/2.
  • 递推：把 (k+1) 提到最前面，剩下正好凑成 (k+2)/2。Step: factor out (k+1); the rest collapses to (k+2)/2.
最常卡壳的地方：k(k+1)/2 + (k+1) 这一步。把公因式 (k+1) 提到最前面 → (k+1)(k/2 + 1) = (k+1)(k+2)/2。先提公因式，剩下的就好算了。Where people get stuck: the line k(k+1)/2 + (k+1). Pull the common factor (k+1) to the front → (k+1)(k/2 + 1) = (k+1)(k+2)/2. Factor first; the rest is easy.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: Assume Σ r (from 1 to k) = k(k+1)/2. For n = k+1, the sum is Σ r (1 to k) + (k+1) = k(k+1)/2 + (k+1) = (k+1)(k+2)/2, as required.
例题Example
用数学归纳法证明：1 + 2 + 3 + … + n = n(n+1)/2 对所有正整数 n 成立。Prove by induction that 1 + 2 + 3 + … + n = n(n+1)/2 for all positive integers n.
① 基础情形：n=1 时，左边=1，右边=1·(1+1)/2=1，成立 ✓。① base case: at n=1, left=1, right=1·(1+1)/2=1 ✓. ② 归纳假设：设 n=k 时成立，即 1+2+…+k = k(k+1)/2。② hypothesis: assume true for n=k, i.e. 1+2+…+k = k(k+1)/2. ③ 归纳步：当 n=k+1 时，1+2+…+k+(k+1) = [k(k+1)/2] + (k+1)（前 k 项套假设）= (k+1)(k/2+1) = (k+1)(k+2)/2。这正是公式里把 n 换成 k+1 的样子，故 n=k+1 也成立。③ step: for n=k+1, 1+2+…+k+(k+1) = [k(k+1)/2] + (k+1) (hypothesis on the first k terms) = (k+1)(k/2+1) = (k+1)(k+2)/2 — exactly the formula with n = k+1, so n=k+1 holds. ④ 由数学归纳法，等式对所有正整数 n 成立。④ by induction, the identity holds for all positive integers n.
练习Practice
归纳步先写「前 k 项 + 第 k+1 项」，再把前 k 项换成假设的式子。Write "first k terms + the (k+1)-th", then swap the first k terms for the hypothesis.

用归纳法证明：1² + 2² + … + n² = n(n+1)(2n+1)/6。（写出基础情形和归纳步的关键变形即可。）Prove by induction: 1² + 2² + … + n² = n(n+1)(2n+1)/6. (Base case and the key step rearrangement suffice.)

点开看答案Show answer
基础：n=1，1²=1，1·2·3/6=1 ✓。
假设 Σk² = k(k+1)(2k+1)/6。
归纳步：1²+…+k²+(k+1)² = k(k+1)(2k+1)/6 + (k+1)² = (k+1)[k(2k+1)/6 + (k+1)] = (k+1)(2k²+7k+6)/6 = (k+1)(k+2)(2k+3)/6，正是 n=k+1 的形式 ✓。 Base: n=1, 1²=1, 1·2·3/6=1 ✓.
Hypothesis: Σk² = k(k+1)(2k+1)/6.
Step: 1²+…+k²+(k+1)² = k(k+1)(2k+1)/6 + (k+1)² = (k+1)[k(2k+1)/6 + (k+1)] = (k+1)(2k²+7k+6)/6 = (k+1)(k+2)(2k+3)/6, the n=k+1 form ✓.
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 证明整除性Induction for divisibility Divisibility by 8证整除性
这一站要你会：用归纳法证明形如 f(n) 能被某数整除的题，掌握核心技巧 f(k+1) − f(k) 的变形。Goal: prove "f(n) is divisible by d" by induction, and master the key trick of rearranging f(k+1) − f(k).
整除题长得吓人，但套路固定。要证 3²ⁿ−1 能被 8 整除，第一步 n=1：3²−1=8 ✓。第二步假设 3²ᵏ−1 能被 8 整除。第三步要证 3²⁽ᵏ⁺¹⁾−1 也能被 8 整除。关键一招：别硬算 3²⁽ᵏ⁺¹⁾−1 本身，而是看「新旧之差」f(k+1) − f(k) = (3²⁽ᵏ⁺¹⁾−1) − (3²ᵏ−1) = 3²ᵏ(3²−1) = 8·3²ᵏ。这个差显然能被 8 整除！既然 f(k) 能被 8 整除（假设），差又能被 8 整除，那 f(k+1) = f(k) + 8·3²ᵏ 自然也能被 8 整除。这就是整除题的「灵魂」。Divisibility questions look scary but follow a fixed routine. To prove 3²ⁿ−1 is divisible by 8, first check n=1: 3²−1=8 ✓. Then assume 3²ᵏ−1 is divisible by 8. The third step shows 3²⁽ᵏ⁺¹⁾−1 is too. The key move: don't compute 3²⁽ᵏ⁺¹⁾−1 directly — look at the "old-minus-new difference" f(k+1) − f(k) = (3²⁽ᵏ⁺¹⁾−1) − (3²ᵏ−1) = 3²ᵏ(3²−1) = 8·3²ᵏ. That difference is clearly divisible by 8! Since f(k) divides by 8 (hypothesis) and the difference does too, f(k+1) = f(k) + 8·3²ᵏ also divides by 8. That is the soul of divisibility proofs.
证 f(n) = 3²ⁿ − 1 能被 8 整除：Prove f(n) = 3²ⁿ − 1 is divisible by 8:
f(k+1) − f(k) = 3²⁽ᵏ⁺¹⁾ − 3²ᵏ = 3²ᵏ(3² − 1) = 8 · 3²ᵏ
  • 基础：n=1，f(1) = 9−1 = 8 ✓。Base: n=1, f(1) = 9−1 = 8 ✓.
  • 假设：f(k) = 3²ᵏ−1 能被 8 整除。Hypothesis: f(k) = 3²ᵏ−1 is divisible by 8.
  • 结论：f(k+1) = f(k) + 8·3²ᵏ，两项都被 8 整除，故 f(k+1) 也被 8 整除。Conclusion: f(k+1) = f(k) + 8·3²ᵏ, both terms divisible by 8, so f(k+1) is too.
通用心法：证整除题，先写出 f(k+1)，再想办法把它拆成「f(k) + 一个明显整除的尾巴」。减法差 f(k+1)−f(k) 往往刚好把碍事的部分抵消，露出因子。General recipe: write f(k+1), then split it as "f(k) + an obviously divisible tail". The difference f(k+1)−f(k) usually cancels the messy part and exposes the factor.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: Assume 3²ᵏ − 1 is divisible by 8. Now 3²⁽ᵏ⁺¹⁾ − 1 = 9·3²ᵏ − 1 = (3²ᵏ − 1) + 8·3²ᵏ. Both terms are divisible by 8, so 3²⁽ᵏ⁺¹⁾ − 1 is divisible by 8.
例题Example
用数学归纳法证明：3²ⁿ − 1 能被 8 整除（对所有正整数 n）。Prove by induction that 3²ⁿ − 1 is divisible by 8 for all positive integers n.
① 基础情形：n=1，3²−1 = 9−1 = 8，能被 8 整除 ✓。① base case: n=1, 3²−1 = 9−1 = 8, divisible by 8 ✓. ② 归纳假设：设 n=k 时 3²ᵏ − 1 能被 8 整除。② hypothesis: assume 3²ᵏ − 1 is divisible by 8. ③ 归纳步：当 n=k+1 时，3²⁽ᵏ⁺¹⁾ − 1 = 9·3²ᵏ − 1 = (3²ᵏ − 1) + 8·3²ᵏ。第一项由假设能被 8 整除，第二项显然含因子 8，故和也能被 8 整除。③ step: for n=k+1, 3²⁽ᵏ⁺¹⁾ − 1 = 9·3²ᵏ − 1 = (3²ᵏ − 1) + 8·3²ᵏ. The first term divides by 8 (hypothesis), the second clearly has factor 8, so the sum divides by 8. ④ 由数学归纳法，3²ⁿ − 1 对所有正整数 n 都能被 8 整除。④ by induction, 3²ⁿ − 1 is divisible by 8 for all positive integers n.
练习Practice
把 f(k+1) 写成 f(k) 加上一个「明显整除」的尾巴。Write f(k+1) as f(k) plus an obviously divisible tail.

用数学归纳法证明：n³ + 2n 能被 3 整除（对所有正整数 n）。Prove by induction that n³ + 2n is divisible by 3 for all positive integers n.

点开看答案Show answer
基础：n=1，1³+2=3 ✓。
设 f(k)=k³+2k 能被 3 整除。
f(k+1) − f(k) = (k+1)³+2(k+1) − (k³+2k) = 3k²+3k+3 = 3(k²+k+1)，能被 3 整除。
故 f(k+1) = f(k) + 3(k²+k+1) 也能被 3 整除 ✓。 Base: n=1, 1³+2=3 ✓.
Let f(k)=k³+2k, divisible by 3.
f(k+1) − f(k) = (k+1)³+2(k+1) − (k³+2k) = 3k²+3k+3 = 3(k²+k+1), divisible by 3.
So f(k+1) = f(k) + 3(k²+k+1) is divisible by 3 ✓.
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 证明矩阵结论Induction for matrices Matrix powers矩阵幂
这一站要你会：用归纳法证明 Mⁿ 的形式（如 Mⁿ = [1 2n; 0 1]），掌握「矩阵乘法 + 套假设」的写法。Goal: prove a form for Mⁿ (e.g. Mⁿ = [1 2n; 0 1]) by induction, combining matrix multiplication with the hypothesis.
矩阵题和级数题是一个骨架，只是把「代数式」换成「矩阵相乘」。要证 M = [1 2; 0 1] 的 n 次方是 [1 2n; 0 1]，先验 n=1：M¹ 显然就是这个矩阵（右上角 2·1=2）✓。假设 n=k 时 Mᵏ = [1 2k; 0 1]。第三步证 n=k+1：Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M，把 Mᵏ 换成假设里的矩阵，再乘上 M，按矩阵乘法一算，右上角 2k+2 = 2(k+1)，正好是 [1 2(k+1); 0 1]。矩阵的归纳题考的其实就是「矩阵乘法基本功 + 套假设」这两样，别被符号吓住。Matrix questions share one skeleton with series questions — just replace "algebra" with "matrix multiplication". To prove the n-th power of M = [1 2; 0 1] is [1 2n; 0 1], first check n=1: M¹ clearly equals that matrix (top-right 2·1=2) ✓. Assume Mᵏ = [1 2k; 0 1] for n=k. The third step shows n=k+1: Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M — swap Mᵏ for the hypothesised matrix, multiply by M, and the top-right entry is 2k+2 = 2(k+1), exactly [1 2(k+1); 0 1]. Matrix induction really just tests "matrix-multiplication basics + apply the hypothesis" — don't let the symbols scare you.
M = [1 2; 0 1] 时，证 Mⁿ = [1 2n; 0 1]：For M = [1 2; 0 1], prove Mⁿ = [1 2n; 0 1]:
Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M = [1 2k; 0 1]·[1 2; 0 1] = [1 2k+2; 0 1] = [1 2(k+1); 0 1] ✓
  • 基础：n=1，M¹ = [1 2; 0 1] ✓。Base: n=1, M¹ = [1 2; 0 1] ✓.
  • 假设：Mᵏ = [1 2k; 0 1]。Hypothesis: Mᵏ = [1 2k; 0 1].
  • 递推：Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M，右上角 2k+2 = 2(k+1)。Step: Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M, top-right 2k+2 = 2(k+1).
最容易错的是乘法顺序：习惯写 Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M。先把假设的 Mᵏ 摆出来，再乘 M，逐项核对右上角那个位置的元素怎么变。Easiest slip is the order: write Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M by habit. Lay out the hypothesised Mᵏ, then multiply by M, and check how the top-right entry changes.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: Assume Mᵏ = [1 2k; 0 1]. Then Mᵏ⁺¹ = MᵏM = [1 2k; 0 1][1 2; 0 1] = [1 2k+2; 0 1] = [1 2(k+1); 0 1], as required.
例题Example
M = [1 2; 0 1]。用数学归纳法证明 Mⁿ = [1 2n; 0 1] 对所有正整数 n 成立。M = [1 2; 0 1]. Prove by induction that Mⁿ = [1 2n; 0 1] for all positive integers n.
① 基础情形：n=1，M¹ = [1 2; 0 1] ✓。① base case: n=1, M¹ = [1 2; 0 1] ✓. ② 归纳假设：设 Mᵏ = [1 2k; 0 1]。② hypothesis: assume Mᵏ = [1 2k; 0 1]. ③ 归纳步：Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M = [1 2k; 0 1]·[1 2; 0 1]。右上角元素 = 1·2 + 2k·1 = 2+2k = 2(k+1)，其余位置不变，故 Mᵏ⁺¹ = [1 2(k+1); 0 1]，正是 n=k+1 的形式。③ step: Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M = [1 2k; 0 1]·[1 2; 0 1]. Top-right entry = 1·2 + 2k·1 = 2+2k = 2(k+1), the rest unchanged, so Mᵏ⁺¹ = [1 2(k+1); 0 1] — the n=k+1 form. ④ 由数学归纳法，Mⁿ = [1 2n; 0 1] 对一切正整数 n 成立。④ by induction, Mⁿ = [1 2n; 0 1] for all positive integers n.
练习Practice
归纳步写 Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M，代入假设的 Mᵏ 再相乘。Write Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M, substitute the hypothesised Mᵏ, then multiply.

已知 M = [1 1; 0 1]。用数学归纳法证明 Mⁿ = [1 n; 0 1]。Given M = [1 1; 0 1], prove by induction that Mⁿ = [1 n; 0 1].

点开看答案Show answer
基础：n=1，M¹ = [1 1; 0 1] ✓。
设 Mᵏ = [1 k; 0 1]。
Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M = [1 k; 0 1]·[1 1; 0 1] = [1 1+k; 0 1] = [1 k+1; 0 1] ✓。 Base: n=1, M¹ = [1 1; 0 1] ✓.
Assume Mᵏ = [1 k; 0 1].
Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M = [1 k; 0 1]·[1 1; 0 1] = [1 1+k; 0 1] = [1 k+1; 0 1] ✓.
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 综合与规范写法Writing a rigorous proof The proof template证明模板
这一站要你会：写出一份「阅卷老师挑不出毛病」的完整归纳证明，规范用语和四步框架一次到位。Goal: write a complete induction proof an examiner can't fault — the four-part frame and standard phrasing in one go.
归纳法证明写得好不好，阅卷老师一眼就看出来。规范写法永远是四段：① 明确写「基础情形」并验算；② 明确写「归纳假设：设 n=k 时成立」；③ 「当 n=k+1 时」开始归纳步，中间一定要出现「由归纳假设」这几个字，让人看出你套用了假设；④ 结尾写「由数学归纳法，命题对所有正整数 n 成立」。中间别跳步，别把 k 和 n 混着写。你就当自己在给老师「讲故事」，每一步都要让读者跟得上。An examiner can tell at a glance whether your induction proof is well written. The proper form is always four parts: ① state "base case" and verify it; ② state "induction hypothesis: assume true for n=k"; ③ begin the step with "when n=k+1", and somewhere inside it must say "by the induction hypothesis" so the reader sees you used it; ④ finish with "by the principle of mathematical induction, the statement holds for all positive integers n". Don't skip steps, and don't mix up k and n. Treat it as telling the examiner a story — every step must let the reader follow.
四步框架 + 规范用语：The four-part frame and standard phrasing:
  • 基础情形：「当 n=1 时，……成立」。Base case: "When n=1, … holds."
  • 归纳假设：「设 n=k 时成立，即 ……」。Hypothesis: "Assume it holds for n=k, i.e. …".
  • 归纳步：「当 n=k+1 时，……（由归纳假设）……，故也成立」。Step: "When n=k+1, … (by the induction hypothesis) …, so it holds."
  • 结论：「由数学归纳法，命题对所有正整数 n 成立」。Conclusion: "By the principle of mathematical induction, it holds for all positive integers n."
按步给分：考试里证明题按步给分，即使归纳步没做全，只要基础情形 + 归纳假设写对了也有分。所以框架宁写全，绝不省略。Marks come per step: in the exam, proof questions are marked by step — even if the step is incomplete, a correct base case and hypothesis still score. So always write the full frame.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The result holds for n = 1. Assume it holds for n = k. When n = k+1, using the induction hypothesis, … . Hence it holds for n = k+1. By the principle of mathematical induction, the result holds for all positive integers n.
例题Example
用规范的归纳法语言，证明 2ⁿ > n 对所有正整数 n 成立。Using standard induction language, prove 2ⁿ > n for all positive integers n.
① 基础情形：n=1，2¹=2 > 1 ✓。① base case: n=1, 2¹=2 > 1 ✓. ② 归纳假设：设 n=k 时 2ᵏ > k。② hypothesis: assume 2ᵏ > k for n=k. ③ 归纳步：当 n=k+1 时，2ᵏ⁺¹ = 2·2ᵏ > 2k（由假设 2ᵏ>k，两边乘 2）。又因为 2k = k+k ≥ k+1（k≥1），所以 2ᵏ⁺¹ > k+1，故 n=k+1 也成立。③ step: when n=k+1, 2ᵏ⁺¹ = 2·2ᵏ > 2k (using 2ᵏ>k, multiply by 2). Since 2k = k+k ≥ k+1 (k≥1), we get 2ᵏ⁺¹ > k+1, so n=k+1 holds. ④ 由数学归纳法，2ⁿ > n 对一切正整数 n 成立。④ by induction, 2ⁿ > n for all positive integers n.
练习Practice
归纳步要把「假设 3ᵏ ≥ 1+2k」真的用上，并补一句为什么 3+6k ≥ 3+2k。In the step, actually use "3ᵏ ≥ 1+2k", and add a line on why 3+6k ≥ 3+2k.

用数学归纳法证明：对一切正整数 n，3ⁿ ≥ 1 + 2n。Prove by induction that 3ⁿ ≥ 1 + 2n for all positive integers n.

点开看答案Show answer
基础：n=1，3¹=3 ≥ 3 ✓。
假设 3ᵏ ≥ 1+2k。
归纳步：3ᵏ⁺¹ = 3·3ᵏ ≥ 3(1+2k) = 3+6k。要证 3+6k ≥ 1+2(k+1)=3+2k，即 4k ≥ 0，对 k≥1 显然成立。
故 3ᵏ⁺¹ ≥ 1+2(k+1) ✓，由归纳法对一切正整数 n 成立。 Base: n=1, 3¹=3 ≥ 3 ✓.
Assume 3ᵏ ≥ 1+2k.
Step: 3ᵏ⁺¹ = 3·3ᵏ ≥ 3(1+2k) = 3+6k. We need 3+6k ≥ 1+2(k+1)=3+2k, i.e. 4k ≥ 0, clearly true for k≥1.
So 3ᵏ⁺¹ ≥ 1+2(k+1) ✓; by induction it holds for all positive integers n.
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —

单元 7 小结Unit 7 summary

① 归纳法 = 多米诺：基础情形（第 1 张倒）+ 归纳步（每张带倒下一张）→ 全部倒。
② 三大题型：级数求和（套假设替换前 k 项）、整除（f(k+1)−f(k) 变形）、矩阵幂（Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M）。
③ 规范四步：基础 → 假设 → 递推 → 结论，别漏「由归纳法」。① induction = dominoes: base case (the first falls) + induction step (each knocks the next) → all fall.
② three question types: series sums (swap the first k terms for the hypothesis), divisibility (rearrange f(k+1)−f(k)), matrix powers (Mᵏ⁺¹ = Mᵏ·M).
③ the four-part frame: base → hypothesis → step → conclusion; don't forget "by induction".

下一站：离开归纳法，进入一个全新的函数家族——双曲函数（sinh、cosh、tanh），看它们和三角函数有多像、又差在哪。Next: leave induction behind and meet a whole new family of functions — the hyperbolic functions (sinh, cosh, tanh), and see how they mirror and differ from the trigonometric ones.

单元 8 · 双曲函数：用 e 指数拼出来的「兄弟版三角函数」Unit 8 · Hyperbolic functions

CAIE 9231 Paper 2 · 5 站 · 用 e 指数拼出来的「兄弟版三角函数」CAIE 9231 Paper 2 · 5 stops · trigonometry's siblings, built out of the exponential e

路线图本单元五站，先认识 sinh、cosh、tanh 三个「兄弟版三角函数」，再学它们的恒等式、反函数，最后会解双曲方程并做微积分Five stops — meet sinh, cosh and tanh (trig's siblings), learn their identities and inverses, then solve hyperbolic equations and do calculus
