建议：第 1 站的 e 指数定义是后面一切的地基，先把 sinh、cosh、tanh 和 eˣ 的关系背熟，后面全是它的变形。Tip: the e-based definitions in stop 1 are the foundation of everything after — learn how sinh, cosh, tanh come from eˣ, and the rest are just reshapes.

1 sinh、cosh、tanh 的定义Definitions of sinh, cosh, tanh definitions定义
这一站要你会：背出 sinh、cosh、tanh 的 e 指数定义，能口算它们在 x = 0 的值。Goal: recall the e-based definitions of sinh, cosh and tanh, and work out their values at x = 0 by hand.
双曲函数长得像三角函数的亲兄弟——名字只差一个 h（sin → sinh），但来历完全不同：三角函数在圆上转出来，双曲函数用e 指数拼出来。你只要记住一个画面：eˣ 和它的倒数 e⁻ˣ，一个往上冲、一个往下掉，sinh 是「冲的减掉掉的再除以 2」，cosh 是「冲的加上掉的再除以 2」，tanh 就是 sinh 除以 cosh。所以它们根本不用背几何图，只用 e 的加减乘除。Hyperbolic functions look like trig's siblings — just one letter h apart (sin → sinh) — but they come from a different place: trig lives on a circle, hyperbolic functions are built from the exponential e. Keep one picture: eˣ rushes up and its reciprocal e⁻ˣ drops down. sinh is "the riser minus the faller, over 2"; cosh is "the riser plus the faller, over 2"; tanh is sinh over cosh. No geometry to memorise — just e, added, subtracted and divided.
三个定义（背熟）：The three definitions (learn them):
sinh x = (eˣ − e⁻ˣ) / 2 cosh x = (eˣ + e⁻ˣ) / 2 tanh x = sinh x / cosh x
  • 读法：sinh 读「shine」，cosh 读「kosh」，tanh 读「than」。Say them: sinh rhymes with "shine", cosh with "cosh", tanh with "than".
  • cosh 永远 ≥ 1，sinh 可正可负，tanh 夹在 −1 和 1 之间。cosh is always ≥ 1, sinh can be any sign, tanh stays between −1 and 1.
别和 sin/cos 混：sin 是「圆的弦」，sinh 是「e 指数的差」。它们名字像、长得也像（都过原点或都有最低点），但公式完全不同——看到 h，就把脑子切到 eˣ。Don't confuse with sin/cos: sin is a chord of a circle, sinh is a difference of exponentials. The names look alike, but the formulas are worlds apart — the moment you see the h, switch your brain to eˣ.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The hyperbolic functions are defined by sinh x = (eˣ − e⁻ˣ)/2 and cosh x = (eˣ + e⁻ˣ)/2, with tanh x = sinh x / cosh x.
例题Example
求 sinh 0、cosh 0 和 tanh 0。Find sinh 0, cosh 0 and tanh 0.
① sinh 0 = (e⁰ − e⁰)/2 = (1 − 1)/2 = 0。① sinh 0 = (e⁰ − e⁰)/2 = (1 − 1)/2 = 0. ② cosh 0 = (e⁰ + e⁰)/2 = (1 + 1)/2 = 1。② cosh 0 = (e⁰ + e⁰)/2 = (1 + 1)/2 = 1. ③ tanh 0 = sinh 0 / cosh 0 = 0/1 = 0。③ tanh 0 = sinh 0 / cosh 0 = 0/1 = 0.
练习Practice
记住 e^(ln a) = a，e^(−ln a) = 1/a。Recall e^(ln a) = a and e^(−ln a) = 1/a.

求 sinh(ln 2) 和 cosh(ln 2)，再算 tanh(ln 2)。Find sinh(ln 2) and cosh(ln 2), then work out tanh(ln 2).

点开看答案Show answer
e^(ln2)=2，e^(−ln2)=1/2。
sinh(ln2) = (2 − 1/2)/2 = (3/2)/2 = 3/4；cosh(ln2) = (2 + 1/2)/2 = (5/2)/2 = 5/4。
tanh(ln2) = (3/4)/(5/4) = 3/5。 e^(ln2)=2, e^(−ln2)=1/2.
sinh(ln2) = (2 − 1/2)/2 = (3/2)/2 = 3/4; cosh(ln2) = (2 + 1/2)/2 = (5/2)/2 = 5/4.
tanh(ln2) = (3/4)/(5/4) = 3/5.
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 双曲恒等式Hyperbolic identities hyperbolic identities双曲恒等式
这一站要你会：记住 cosh²x − sinh²x = 1 这条核心恒等式，以及 sinh 2x、cosh 2x 的展开式。Goal: recall the key identity cosh²x − sinh²x = 1, plus the expansions of sinh 2x and cosh 2x.
最核心的一条是 cosh²x − sinh²x = 1。它和三角的 cos²+sin²=1 只差一个符号——加号换成了减号。为什么？把 cosh 和 sinh 的 e 定义代进去一减，eˣ 和 e⁻ˣ 交叉项刚好抵消，剩下 1，就这么干脆。倍角公式也几乎照搬：sinh 2x = 2 sinh x cosh x（和 sin 2x 一模一样）；cosh 2x 则是 cosh² 加 sinh²（注意是加，因为符号颠倒了）。The headline identity is cosh²x − sinh²x = 1. It differs from cos²+sin²=1 by exactly one sign — the plus becomes a minus. Why? Substitute the e-definitions of cosh and sinh and subtract: the cross terms cancel perfectly, leaving 1, that clean. The double-angle formulas follow almost verbatim: sinh 2x = 2 sinh x cosh x (identical to sin 2x); cosh 2x is cosh² plus sinh² (note the plus, because the signs flip).
三条核心恒等式：Three core identities:
cosh²x − sinh²x = 1 sinh 2x = 2 sinh x cosh x cosh 2x = cosh²x + sinh²x = 2cosh²x − 1 = 1 + 2sinh²x
  • 对照记忆：三角「+」变双曲「−」；凡有两个 sinh 相乘的项，符号要翻。Memory map: trig "+" becomes hyperbolic "−"; any term with two sinh factors flips its sign.
Osborn 法则：把一条三角恒等式里的 sin 换成 sinh 时，凡是出现「两个 sinh 相乘」（比如 sin²x → sinh²x）的项都要变号。cos²+sin²=1 里 sin² 是「两个 sinh 相乘」，所以变号成 cosh² − sinh² = 1。Osborn's rule: when you replace sin by sinh in a trig identity, any term with a product of two sinh's (like sin²x → sinh²x) must change sign. In cos²+sin²=1 the sin² is a product of two sinh's, so it flips to cosh² − sinh² = 1.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The fundamental hyperbolic identity is cosh²x − sinh²x = 1, obtained by substituting the exponential definitions. The double-angle formulas are sinh 2x = 2 sinh x cosh x and cosh 2x = cosh²x + sinh²x.
例题Example
已知 sinh x = 3/4，用恒等式求 cosh x。Given sinh x = 3/4, use the identity to find cosh x.
① 用 cosh²x − sinh²x = 1 → cosh²x = 1 + sinh²x。① from cosh²x − sinh²x = 1 → cosh²x = 1 + sinh²x. ② cosh²x = 1 + (3/4)² = 1 + 9/16 = 25/16。② cosh²x = 1 + (3/4)² = 1 + 9/16 = 25/16. ③ cosh x = 5/4（cosh 恒为正，取正根）。③ cosh x = 5/4 (cosh is always positive, take the positive root).
练习Practice
用 cosh²x − sinh²x = 1 解出 sinh²x，注意 sinh 可正可负。Use cosh²x − sinh²x = 1 to get sinh²x; note sinh can be either sign.

已知 cosh x = 5/3，求 sinh x。Given cosh x = 5/3, find sinh x.

点开看答案Show answer
sinh²x = cosh²x − 1 = 25/9 − 1 = 16/9。
所以 sinh x = ±4/3——cosh 决定不了 sinh 的正负（sinh 是奇函数，需要额外条件才能定号）。 sinh²x = cosh²x − 1 = 25/9 − 1 = 16/9.
So sinh x = ±4/3 — cosh cannot fix the sign of sinh (sinh is odd; you need extra information to choose).
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 反双曲函数Inverse hyperbolic functions inverse hyperbolic反双曲
这一站要你会：写出 arsinh、arcosh、artanh 的对数形式，并记住它们的定义域。Goal: write arsinh, arcosh and artanh in logarithmic form, and know their domains.
反双曲函数最妙的地方：它们不是新函数，就是普通的 ln 对数。arsinh 是 sinh 的反函数——「给一个函数值，反过来问 x 是多少」。因为 sinh、cosh 本来是用 e 指数定义的，反解时自然冒出 ln。比如 arsinh x = ln(x + √(x²+1))，看着吓人，其实只是「解 e 方程再取对数」的产物。注意名字里的 a 是 area（面积）不是 arc——小细节，考判断题会考。The neatest thing about inverse hyperbolic functions: they aren't new functions at all — just ordinary ln. arsinh is the inverse of sinh — "given a value, ask what x made it". Since sinh and cosh were defined from e in the first place, inverting them naturally spits out ln. For example arsinh x = ln(x + √(x²+1)); it looks scary but is just "solve the e-equation, then take the log". Note the a stands for area, not arc — a small detail that shows up in true/false questions.
三个对数形式（考试要默写）：The three logarithmic forms (write from memory):
arsinh x = ln(x + √(x² + 1)) arcosh x = ln(x + √(x² − 1)) artanh x = ½ ln((1 + x)/(1 − x))
  • 定义域：arsinh 对任意 x；arcosh 要 x ≥ 1；artanh 要 |x| < 1。Domains: arsinh for all x; arcosh needs x ≥ 1; artanh needs |x| < 1.
  • 特殊值：arsinh 0 = 0，arcosh 1 = 0，artanh 0 = 0。Special values: arsinh 0 = 0, arcosh 1 = 0, artanh 0 = 0.
根号里看清加减：arsinh 是 √(x²+1)（加 1），arcosh 是 √(x²−1)（减 1）。背成一句话：「arsinh 加一，arcosh 减一」。Watch the sign in the root: arsinh has √(x²+1) (plus 1), arcosh has √(x²−1) (minus 1). A one-liner: "arsinh plus one, arcosh minus one".
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The inverse hyperbolic functions are given by arsinh x = ln(x + √(x²+1)), arcosh x = ln(x + √(x²−1)) and artanh x = ½ ln((1+x)/(1−x)).
例题Example
验证 arsinh 0 = 0，并求 arsinh 1（答案留 ln 形式）。Verify arsinh 0 = 0, and find arsinh 1 (leave your answer in ln form).
① arsinh 0 = ln(0 + √(0²+1)) = ln 1 = 0。① arsinh 0 = ln(0 + √(0²+1)) = ln 1 = 0. ② arsinh 1 = ln(1 + √(1²+1)) = ln(1 + √2)。② arsinh 1 = ln(1 + √(1²+1)) = ln(1 + √2).
练习Practice
直接代入对数公式，记住 ln 1 = 0。Substitute into the logarithmic formulas; recall ln 1 = 0.

求 arcosh 1 和 artanh 0。Find arcosh 1 and artanh 0.

点开看答案Show answer
arcosh 1 = ln(1 + √(1−1)) = ln(1+0) = ln 1 = 0。
artanh 0 = ½ ln((1+0)/(1−0)) = ½ ln 1 = 0。 arcosh 1 = ln(1 + √(1−1)) = ln(1+0) = ln 1 = 0.
artanh 0 = ½ ln((1+0)/(1−0)) = ½ ln 1 = 0.
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 解双曲方程Solving hyperbolic equations solving hyperbolic equations解双曲方程
这一站要你会：会用「换 e 指数 + 令 y = eˣ」解含 sinh、cosh 的方程。Goal: solve equations with sinh and cosh by switching to exponentials and letting y = eˣ.
双曲方程看着陌生，其实是一条换了马甲的 e 指数方程。遇到 sinh 或 cosh，别慌，先把它们按定义拆成 eˣ 和 e⁻ˣ；拆完你会发现 e⁻ˣ 很碍事，那就令 y = eˣ，于是 e⁻ˣ 变成 1/y，方程就成了一个普通的 y 的二次方程。解出 y，别忘了 eˣ 永远是正的，负根直接扔掉，最后 x = ln y 收工。A hyperbolic equation looks unfamiliar but is just an exponential equation in disguise. See a sinh or cosh? Don't panic — first unpack it into eˣ and e⁻ˣ using the definitions. The e⁻ˣ is the annoying part, so let y = eˣ; then e⁻ˣ becomes 1/y and the equation turns into an ordinary quadratic in y. Solve it, remember eˣ is always positive so drop any negative root, and finish with x = ln y.
两种解法，按情况选：Two methods, pick by situation:
  • 换 e 指数：把 sinh、cosh 全写成 eˣ、e⁻ˣ，再令 y = eˣ 换成二次方程——最通用。Switch to exponentials: write sinh/cosh in eˣ, e⁻ˣ, then let y = eˣ to get a quadratic — the most general.
  • 直接反函数：形如 sinh x = a 的，直接 x = arsinh a = ln(a + √(a²+1))。Straight inverse: for sinh x = a, just x = arsinh a = ln(a + √(a²+1)).
别忘 eˣ > 0：换元解出 y 后，负根一定要舍去（e 的任何次方都为正），只保留正根再取 ln。Don't forget eˣ > 0: after solving for y, discard any negative root (e to any power is positive), keep only the positive one before taking ln.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: To solve the equation, write sinh and cosh in exponential form, substitute y = eˣ, solve the resulting quadratic, discard the negative root since eˣ > 0, and take logarithms.
例题Example
解方程 sinh x = 2。Solve the equation sinh x = 2.
① sinh x = 2 → (eˣ − e⁻ˣ)/2 = 2 → eˣ − e⁻ˣ = 4。① sinh x = 2 → (eˣ − e⁻ˣ)/2 = 2 → eˣ − e⁻ˣ = 4. ② 令 y = eˣ：y − 1/y = 4 → 两边乘 y → y² − 4y − 1 = 0。② let y = eˣ: y − 1/y = 4 → multiply by y → y² − 4y − 1 = 0. ③ y = (4 ± √(16+4))/2 = 2 ± √5，舍去负根 2−√5（≈ −0.24 < 0）。③ y = (4 ± √(16+4))/2 = 2 ± √5, drop the negative root 2−√5 (≈ −0.24 < 0). ④ x = ln(2 + √5)。④ x = ln(2 + √5).
练习Practice
cosh 换成 e 指数后是「加号」，得到的二次方程会有两个正根。cosh unpacks with a plus sign; the quadratic will have two positive roots.

解方程 cosh x = 2。Solve the equation cosh x = 2.

点开看答案Show answer
cosh x = 2 → (eˣ + e⁻ˣ)/2 = 2 → eˣ + e⁻ˣ = 4。
令 y = eˣ：y + 1/y = 4 → y² − 4y + 1 = 0。
y = (4 ± √(16−4))/2 = 2 ± √3（两个都是正根）。
所以 x = ln(2+√3) 或 ln(2−√3)（后者为负，因为 2−√3 ≈ 0.27 < 1）。 cosh x = 2 → (eˣ + e⁻ˣ)/2 = 2 → eˣ + e⁻ˣ = 4.
Let y = eˣ: y + 1/y = 4 → y² − 4y + 1 = 0.
y = (4 ± √(16−4))/2 = 2 ± √3 (both positive roots).
So x = ln(2+√3) or ln(2−√3) (the latter is negative, since 2−√3 ≈ 0.27 < 1).
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 微分与积分Differentiation and integration differentiation & integration微分积分
这一站要你会：记住 sinh、cosh、tanh 的导数与积分，并能从 e 定义出发推导。Goal: recall the derivatives and integrals of sinh, cosh and tanh, and derive them from the e-definitions.
双曲函数的微积分，好消息是特别整齐：sinh 求导是 cosh，cosh 求导是 sinh——互相换着来，没有三角里那个烦人的负号（你记得 d/dx cos x = −sin x 吧？双曲里负号消失了）。为什么？因为 sinh = (eˣ−e⁻ˣ)/2，求导时 e⁻ˣ 变成 −e⁻ˣ，那个「−(−)」刚好把减号转成加号，于是原封不动变出 cosh。积分就是倒着走：∫ sinh = cosh + C。The good news about hyperbolic calculus: it is beautifully tidy. sinh differentiates to cosh, and cosh to sinh — they swap into each other with none of trig's annoying minus sign (remember d/dx cos x = −sin x? In the hyperbolic world that minus disappears). Why? Since sinh = (eˣ−e⁻ˣ)/2, differentiating turns e⁻ˣ into −e⁻ˣ, and that "−(−)" flips the minus into a plus, giving cosh unchanged. Integration runs backwards: ∫ sinh = cosh + C.
导数与积分（背熟）：Derivatives and integrals (learn them):
d/dx sinh x = cosh x ∫ sinh x dx = cosh x + C d/dx cosh x = sinh x ∫ cosh x dx = sinh x + C d/dx tanh x = sech²x ∫ sech²x dx = tanh x + C
  • sech x = 1/cosh x，所以 tanh 的导数是 1/cosh²x。sech x = 1/cosh x, so the derivative of tanh is 1/cosh²x.
最常错的一点：cosh 求导是 +sinh，不是 −sinh。和 d/dx cos = −sin 对比，双曲版把负号「吃掉」了。Top mistake: the derivative of cosh is +sinh, not −sinh. Compared with d/dx cos = −sin, the hyperbolic version "eats" the minus sign.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: Differentiating sinh x = (eˣ − e⁻ˣ)/2 gives cosh x = (eˣ + e⁻ˣ)/2, so d/dx sinh x = cosh x. Similarly d/dx cosh x = sinh x and ∫ sinh x dx = cosh x + C.
例题Example
从 e 指数定义出发，证明 d/dx sinh x = cosh x。Starting from the exponential definition, prove that d/dx sinh x = cosh x.
① sinh x = (eˣ − e⁻ˣ)/2。① sinh x = (eˣ − e⁻ˣ)/2. ② d/dx eˣ = eˣ，d/dx e⁻ˣ = −e⁻ˣ（链式法则）。② d/dx eˣ = eˣ, d/dx e⁻ˣ = −e⁻ˣ (chain rule). ③ d/dx sinh x = (eˣ − (−e⁻ˣ))/2 = (eˣ + e⁻ˣ)/2 = cosh x。③ d/dx sinh x = (eˣ − (−e⁻ˣ))/2 = (eˣ + e⁻ˣ)/2 = cosh x.
练习Practice
照例题的套路，把 cosh 拆成 e 指数再求导。Follow the example: unpack cosh into exponentials, then differentiate.

求 d/dx cosh x 和 ∫ sinh x dx。Find d/dx cosh x and ∫ sinh x dx.

点开看答案Show answer
d/dx cosh x = (eˣ + (−e⁻ˣ))/2 = (eˣ − e⁻ˣ)/2 = sinh x。
∫ sinh x dx = ∫ (eˣ − e⁻ˣ)/2 dx = (eˣ + e⁻ˣ)/2 + C = cosh x + C。 d/dx cosh x = (eˣ + (−e⁻ˣ))/2 = (eˣ − e⁻ˣ)/2 = sinh x.
∫ sinh x dx = ∫ (eˣ − e⁻ˣ)/2 dx = (eˣ + e⁻ˣ)/2 + C = cosh x + C.
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —

单元 8 小结Unit 8 summary

① 定义：sinh x = (eˣ−e⁻ˣ)/2，cosh x = (eˣ+e⁻ˣ)/2，tanh x = sinh/cosh。
② 恒等式：cosh²x − sinh²x = 1（三角的「+」变「−」）；sinh 2x = 2sinh x cosh x，cosh 2x = cosh²x + sinh²x。
③ 反函数：arsinh x = ln(x+√(x²+1))，arcosh x = ln(x+√(x²−1))，artanh x = ½ln((1+x)/(1−x))。
④ 解方程：换成 e 指数、令 y = eˣ、解二次、取正根、再 ln。
⑤ 微积分：d/dx sinh = cosh、d/dx cosh = sinh，∫ sinh = cosh + C——没有负号。① definitions: sinh x = (eˣ−e⁻ˣ)/2, cosh x = (eˣ+e⁻ˣ)/2, tanh x = sinh/cosh.
② identities: cosh²x − sinh²x = 1 (trig's "+" becomes "−"); sinh 2x = 2sinh x cosh x, cosh 2x = cosh²x + sinh²x.
③ inverses: arsinh x = ln(x+√(x²+1)), arcosh x = ln(x+√(x²−1)), artanh x = ½ln((1+x)/(1−x)).
④ solving: switch to exponentials, let y = eˣ, solve the quadratic, keep the positive root, take ln.
⑤ calculus: d/dx sinh = cosh, d/dx cosh = sinh, ∫ sinh = cosh + C — no minus sign.

下一站：矩阵进阶——把普通矩阵的工具再往深挖一层，学特征值、特征向量和对角化。Next: matrices II — dig one level deeper into matrices with eigenvalues, eigenvectors and diagonalisation.

单元 9 · 矩阵进阶 Matrices：特征值与特征向量Unit 9 · Matrices II: eigenvalues and eigenvectors

CAIE 9231 Paper 2 · 5 站 · 找矩阵的「固有方向」和「固有伸缩倍数」CAIE 9231 Paper 2 · 5 stops · find a matrix's own directions and its built-in stretch factors

路线图本单元五站，先弄懂「特征值 = 固有伸缩倍数、特征向量 = 固有方向」，再学会把它们一个个求出来，最后用对角化轻松算矩阵的高次幂Five stops — grasp "eigenvalue = built-in stretch, eigenvector = built-in direction", learn how to find them, then use diagonalisation to compute high powers of a matrix

  1. 特征值与特征向量Eigenvalues and eigenvectors
  2. 求特征值Finding eigenvalues
  3. 求特征向量Finding eigenvectors
  4. 对角化Diagonalisation
  5. 应用Applications

建议：第 1 站的「Av = λv」是整单元的灵魂——先把它读成「变换后方向不变、只被拉长 λ 倍」这句话，后面求值、求向量、对角化全都围着它转。Tip: "Av = λv" in stop 1 is the soul of the unit — read it as "the direction is unchanged, just stretched by λ", and everything after (finding values, finding vectors, diagonalising) orbits around it.

1 特征值与特征向量Eigenvalues and eigenvectors Av = λv固有方向
这一站要你会：说清 Av = λv 的含义，会验证一个向量是不是某矩阵的特征向量、对应的特征值是多少。Goal: explain what Av = λv means, and check whether a vector is an eigenvector of a matrix, finding its eigenvalue.
单元 4 我们学过「矩阵是点的搬运工」，但有个问题一直没问：有没有哪些方向，被搬完之后方向一点没变，只是被拉长或压短？有！这些方向就是特征向量，拉长或压短的倍数就是特征值。一句话记住：A·v = λv——矩阵乘上去，等于一个数 λ 乘上去，也就是「方向不变、长度乘 λ」。λ 可以大于 1（拉长）、介于 0 和 1（压短）、甚至是负数（掉头 180°）。找到它们意义重大：矩阵再复杂，在它「固有方向」上，其实都只是在做最简单的拉伸。In Unit 4 we learned that "a matrix is a point mover", but one question never came up: are there directions that, after being moved, keep their direction exactly and are only stretched or squashed? Yes! Those directions are the eigenvectors, and the stretch factor is the eigenvalue. Remember it in one line: A·v = λv — multiplying by the matrix equals multiplying by the number λ, i.e. "same direction, length × λ". λ can be bigger than 1 (stretch), between 0 and 1 (squash), or even negative (a 180° flip). Finding them matters hugely: however complicated A is, along its "own directions" it is doing nothing but the simplest stretch.
  • 定义：A·v = λv（v ≠ 0）时，λ 是特征值，v 是特征向量。Definition: if A·v = λv (v ≠ 0), then λ is an eigenvalue and v an eigenvector.
  • 方向不变：A·v 与 v 共线（在一条直线上）。Same direction: A·v and v are collinear (on one line).
  • λ 的含义：λ > 1 拉长；0 < λ < 1 压短；λ < 0 反向。What λ means: λ > 1 stretches, 0 < λ < 1 squashes, λ < 0 flips.
v 不能是零向量：零向量乘任何矩阵都得零，等于 λ·0，什么也说明不了。所以定义里特别写 v ≠ 0。v must not be the zero vector: the zero vector times any matrix is zero, equal to λ·0, which proves nothing. That is why the definition insists v ≠ 0.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: A nonzero vector v is an eigenvector of A with eigenvalue λ if A·v = λv. Geometrically, A stretches v by a factor λ without changing its direction.
例题Example
设 A = [2 1; 0 3]。验证 v = [1; 0] 是 A 的特征向量，并求出对应的特征值。Let A = [2 1; 0 3]. Verify that v = [1; 0] is an eigenvector of A, and find its eigenvalue.
① 算 A·v = [2 1; 0 3][1; 0] = [2·1 + 1·0; 0·1 + 3·0] = [2; 0]。① compute A·v = [2 1; 0 3][1; 0] = [2·1 + 1·0; 0·1 + 3·0] = [2; 0]. ② 而 2·v = 2[1; 0] = [2; 0]。② and 2·v = 2[1; 0] = [2; 0]. ③ 所以 A·v = 2v，v 是特征向量，特征值 λ = 2。③ so A·v = 2v: v is an eigenvector with eigenvalue λ = 2.
练习Practice
算 A·v，看它是不是 v 的某个常数倍。Work out A·v and see if it is a constant multiple of v.

设 A = [2 1; 0 3]。验证 v = [1; 1] 是 A 的特征向量，并求出对应的特征值。Let A = [2 1; 0 3]. Verify that v = [1; 1] is an eigenvector, and find its eigenvalue.

点开看答案Show answer
A[1; 1] = [2·1 + 1·1; 0·1 + 3·1] = [3; 3] = 3[1; 1]。
所以 v = [1; 1] 是特征向量，特征值 λ = 3。 A[1; 1] = [2·1 + 1·1; 0·1 + 3·1] = [3; 3] = 3[1; 1].
so v = [1; 1] is an eigenvector with eigenvalue λ = 3.
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 求特征值Finding eigenvalues det(A−λI)=0特征方程
这一站要你会：写出特征方程 det(A − λI) = 0，并把它解出来得到所有特征值。Goal: write down the characteristic equation det(A − λI) = 0 and solve it for all the eigenvalues.
怎么把 λ 找出来？就三步：① 把 A 对角线上的每个数都减去 λ，得到 A − λI（注意非对角元素 1 和 0 原样不动）；② 令它的行列式等于 0；③ 解这个方程。为什么是「行列式 = 0」这一步？因为 det(A−λI)=0 正好等价于「(A−λI)v=0 有非零解」，也就是「存在方向不变的特征向量」。对 2×2 矩阵，展开 det 会得到一个关于 λ 的二次方程——名字吓人叫「特征方程」，本质就是你早就会解的一元二次方程，别被唬住。How do you find λ? Three steps: ① subtract λ from every diagonal entry of A to get A − λI (the off-diagonal entries 1 and 0 stay put); ② set its determinant equal to 0; ③ solve. Why "determinant = 0"? Because det(A−λI)=0 is exactly equivalent to "(A−λI)v=0 has a nonzero solution", i.e. "there is a direction-preserving eigenvector". For a 2×2 matrix, expanding the det gives a quadratic in λ — a scary name ("characteristic equation") for a plain one-variable quadratic you solved long ago. Don't let it intimidate you.
特征方程（2×2 通式）：The characteristic equation (2×2 general form):
det(A − λI) = 0 对于 A = [a b; c d]：det[a−λ b; c d−λ] = (a−λ)(d−λ) − bc = 0
  • 展开后是 λ 的二次方程，最多有 2 个特征值（可能相同）。When expanded this is a quadratic in λ, with at most two eigenvalues (possibly equal).
减 λ 只加在对角线上：非对角元素 b、c 原样保留。常见错是把 [2 1; 0 3] 减成 [2−λ 1−λ; 0−λ 3−λ]，这是错的——只有 2 和 3 这两个对角元素要减 λ。Subtract λ only on the diagonal: the off-diagonal entries b, c stay unchanged. A common mistake is turning [2 1; 0 3] into [2−λ 1−λ; 0−λ 3−λ] — wrong; only the diagonal entries 2 and 3 lose a λ.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The eigenvalues of A satisfy the characteristic equation det(A − λI) = 0. For a 2×2 matrix this is a quadratic equation in λ, giving at most two eigenvalues.
例题Example
求 A = [2 1; 0 3] 的特征值。Find the eigenvalues of A = [2 1; 0 3].
① A − λI = [2−λ 1; 0 3−λ]。① A − λI = [2−λ 1; 0 3−λ]. ② det = (2−λ)(3−λ) − 1·0 = (2−λ)(3−λ)。② det = (2−λ)(3−λ) − 1·0 = (2−λ)(3−λ). ③ 令 det = 0：(2−λ)(3−λ) = 0 → 特征值 λ = 2 或 λ = 3。③ set det = 0: (2−λ)(3−λ) = 0 → eigenvalues λ = 2 or λ = 3.
练习Practice
先减 λ，再令 det = 0，展开成二次方程。Subtract λ, set det = 0, and expand into a quadratic.

求 B = [4 1; 2 3] 的特征值。Find the eigenvalues of B = [4 1; 2 3].

点开看答案Show answer
det(B − λI) = (4−λ)(3−λ) − 1·2 = λ² − 7λ + 10 = (λ−2)(λ−5)。
所以特征值 λ = 2 或 λ = 5。 det(B − λI) = (4−λ)(3−λ) − 1·2 = λ² − 7λ + 10 = (λ−2)(λ−5).
so the eigenvalues are λ = 2 or λ = 5.
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 求特征向量Finding eigenvectors (A−λI)v = 0齐次方程组
这一站要你会：解齐次方程组 (A−λI)v = 0 求出特征向量，并明白特征向量可差一个常数倍。Goal: solve the homogeneous system (A−λI)v = 0 to find the eigenvectors, and understand they are only defined up to a constant multiple.
有了 λ，第二步把它代回 (A−λI)v = 0。这是个齐次方程组——右边全是 0，解起来有个特点：解不唯一，通常有一个变量「随便取」，剩下的跟着它走。这正是特征向量的天性：它只在乎方向，不在乎长短，所以 v 乘上任何非零常数还是特征向量，就像一束激光，指哪儿比打多远更重要。考试一般取个最顺眼的——让某个分量 = 1，或取整数——写成 v = [1; 0] 这类形式。最后提醒：每个特征值对应一套特征向量，λ = 2 的和 λ = 3 的要分开求，别混在一起。Once you have λ, the second step is to substitute it back into (A−λI)v = 0. This is a homogeneous system — all zeros on the right — and it has a signature: the solution is not unique. Usually one variable is "free" and the rest follow it. That is the eigenvector's nature: it cares about direction, not length, so any nonzero multiple of v is still an eigenvector — like a laser beam, where pointing matters more than range. In exams pick the tidiest form — set one component to 1, or take integers — and write v = [1; 0]. Finally: each eigenvalue has its own set of eigenvectors; find λ = 2's and λ = 3's separately, don't mix them.
  • 步骤：λ 代回 → 解 (A−λI)v = 0 → 得到特征向量。Steps: substitute λ → solve (A−λI)v = 0 → get the eigenvector.
  • 为什么总有解：det(A−λI)=0 保证这齐次方程组有无穷多解（一个自由变量）。Why solutions exist: det(A−λI)=0 guarantees this homogeneous system has infinitely many solutions (one free variable).
  • 可差常数倍：v 和 k·v（k ≠ 0）都是同一特征值的特征向量。Up to a multiple: v and k·v (k ≠ 0) are both eigenvectors for the same eigenvalue.
验算妙招：解出 v 后，直接算 A·v，看它是否等于 λ·v。等于就稳了，一眼能抓住算错的 v。A quick check: after finding v, compute A·v and see if it equals λ·v. If so you are safe; if not, you have caught a mistake instantly.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: For each eigenvalue λ, solve the homogeneous system (A − λI)v = 0 to find the eigenvectors. The solution is not unique — any nonzero scalar multiple of an eigenvector is also an eigenvector.
例题Example
设 A = [2 1; 0 3]。求特征值 λ = 2 对应的特征向量。Let A = [2 1; 0 3]. Find the eigenvector corresponding to λ = 2.
① λ = 2 → A − 2I = [0 1; 0 1]。① λ = 2 → A − 2I = [0 1; 0 1]. ② (A−2I)v = [0 1; 0 1][x; y] = [y; y] = [0; 0] → y = 0。② (A−2I)v = [0 1; 0 1][x; y] = [y; y] = [0; 0] → y = 0. ③ x 任意 → v = [x; 0] = x[1; 0]，取 v = [1; 0]。③ x is free → v = [x; 0] = x[1; 0]; take v = [1; 0].
练习Practice
λ = 3 代回，解 (A−3I)v = 0。Substitute λ = 3 and solve (A−3I)v = 0.

设 A = [2 1; 0 3]。求特征值 λ = 3 对应的特征向量。Let A = [2 1; 0 3]. Find the eigenvector corresponding to λ = 3.

点开看答案Show answer
A − 3I = [−1 1; 0 0]。
(−1)x + y = 0 → y = x，所以 v = [x; x] = x[1; 1]，取 v = [1; 1]。 A − 3I = [−1 1; 0 0].
(−1)x + y = 0 → y = x, so v = [x; x] = x[1; 1]; take v = [1; 1].
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 对角化Diagonalisation A = PDP⁻¹对角化
这一站要你会：用特征值、特征向量写出对角化 A = PDP⁻¹，并会算 Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹。Goal: write the diagonalisation A = PDP⁻¹ from the eigenvalues and eigenvectors, and compute Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹.
对角化是特征值最赚钱的用法。把 A 的两个特征向量并排塞进 P 的列，把特征值按同样顺序放到 D 的对角线，再补上 P 的逆，就得到 A = PDP⁻¹。为什么值钱？因为算 A 的高次方会变得轻松：A² = (PDP⁻¹)(PDP⁻¹)，中间 P⁻¹P = I 一抵消，只剩 PD²P⁻¹；一路推到 n 次，就是 Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹。而 Dⁿ 几乎免费——把 D 对角线上的数各自取 n 次方就行。本来要连乘 n 次矩阵，现在只剩「左乘 P、右乘 P⁻¹」两次。Diagonalisation is where eigenvalues pay off. Pack A's two eigenvectors side by side into the columns of P, put the eigenvalues in the same order down the diagonal of D, and add the inverse of P — there it is: A = PDP⁻¹. Why is it valuable? Because high powers of A become easy: A² = (PDP⁻¹)(PDP⁻¹), the middle P⁻¹P cancels to I, leaving PD²P⁻¹; push this all the way to the n-th power and you get Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹. And Dⁿ is nearly free — just raise each diagonal entry of D to the power n. Instead of n matrix multiplications, only "multiply by P on the left and P⁻¹ on the right" remain.
  • 对角化：A = PDP⁻¹，P 的列 = 特征向量，D 的对角线 = 特征值。Diagonalisation: A = PDP⁻¹, where P's columns are the eigenvectors and D's diagonal holds the eigenvalues.
  • 矩阵的幂：Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹，其中 Dⁿ = diag(λ₁ⁿ, λ₂ⁿ)。Powers: Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹, where Dⁿ = diag(λ₁ⁿ, λ₂ⁿ).
  • 前提：特征向量线性无关（2×2 有 2 个不同特征值就一定够）。Condition: the eigenvectors must be linearly independent (two distinct eigenvalues for a 2×2 always suffice).
顺序要对应：P 第 1 列放哪个特征向量，D 对角线第 1 个就放它的特征值。顺序一乱，P 和 D 对不上，等式就不成立。Match the order: whichever eigenvector sits in column 1 of P, its eigenvalue must sit in position (1,1) of D. If the order slips, P and D disagree and the equation fails.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: If P is the matrix whose columns are the eigenvectors of A, and D is the diagonal matrix of the corresponding eigenvalues, then A = PDP⁻¹, and hence Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹.
例题Example
对 A = [2 1; 0 3] 写出对角化 A = PDP⁻¹，并由此给出 Aⁿ。For A = [2 1; 0 3], write the diagonalisation A = PDP⁻¹, and hence give Aⁿ.
① 特征向量 [1; 0]、[1; 1]，对应特征值 2、3。① eigenvectors [1; 0], [1; 1] with eigenvalues 2, 3. ② P = [1 1; 0 1]，D = [2 0; 0 3]，P⁻¹ = [1 −1; 0 1]。② P = [1 1; 0 1], D = [2 0; 0 3], P⁻¹ = [1 −1; 0 1]. ③ 所以 A = PDP⁻¹。③ so A = PDP⁻¹. ④ Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹ = [1 1; 0 1][2ⁿ 0; 0 3ⁿ][1 −1; 0 1]。④ Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹ = [1 1; 0 1][2ⁿ 0; 0 3ⁿ][1 −1; 0 1].
练习Practice
先算 D²，再夹在 P 和 P⁻¹ 中间乘出来。Find D² first, then sandwich it between P and P⁻¹.

用对角化求 A = [2 1; 0 3] 的 A²，再用直接乘法验算。Use diagonalisation to find A² for A = [2 1; 0 3], then check by direct multiplication.

点开看答案Show answer
D² = [4 0; 0 9]。
A² = PD²P⁻¹ = [1 1; 0 1][4 0; 0 9][1 −1; 0 1] = [4 9; 0 9][1 −1; 0 1] = [4 5; 0 9]。
直接乘：[2 1; 0 3][2 1; 0 3] = [4, 2+3; 0, 9] = [4 5; 0 9] ✓。 D² = [4 0; 0 9].
A² = PD²P⁻¹ = [1 1; 0 1][4 0; 0 9][1 −1; 0 1] = [4 9; 0 9][1 −1; 0 1] = [4 5; 0 9].
Direct: [2 1; 0 3][2 1; 0 3] = [4, 2+3; 0, 9] = [4 5; 0 9] ✓.
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 应用Applications invariant lines不变线
这一站要你会：用特征值判断变换性质（不变线），并会算矩阵的幂。Goal: use eigenvalues to read a transformation's nature (invariant lines), and compute matrix powers.
学完前四站，最后把两件事串起来用。第一件：判断变换、找不变线。特征向量天生「方向不变」，所以每个特征向量指的方向就是一条不变线——被 A 变换后还落在原来那条直线上。特征值的正负还能告诉你它翻不翻面：λ > 0 是纯拉伸，λ < 0 是「先拉伸再掉头 180°」。第二件：算矩阵的高次幂。考试常问 A⁵、A¹⁰⁰ 这种，直接乘会乘到天荒地老；但对角化之后 Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹，Dⁿ 只要把对角线上的数各自取 n 次方，两步就搞定。一个工具，两头都派得上用场。After the first four stops, tie the two threads together. First: read the transformation and find invariant lines. An eigenvector is "direction-preserving" by nature, so the direction it points along is an invariant line — after A acts, points on it land back on the same line. The sign of the eigenvalue even tells you whether it flips: λ > 0 is a pure stretch, λ < 0 is "stretch then turn 180°". Second: compute high powers. Exams love A⁵ or A¹⁰⁰; multiplying directly would take forever, but after diagonalising, Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹ and Dⁿ just raises each diagonal entry to the power n — two steps and done. One tool, useful at both ends.
  • 不变线：特征向量方向，满足 A·v = λv，方向不变。Invariant line: the direction of an eigenvector, with A·v = λv, direction preserved.
  • λ 的符号：λ > 0 拉伸；λ < 0 反向拉伸；|λ| < 1 压缩。Sign of λ: λ > 0 stretches, λ < 0 stretches with a flip, |λ| < 1 compresses.
  • 矩阵幂：Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹，Dⁿ 对角线各取 n 次方。Matrix powers: Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹, with Dⁿ raising each diagonal entry to the n.
判「是不是不变线」最快的方法：找一条直线，看上面任一点变换后是否还在这条线上。特征向量给出的正是这种方向。Fastest way to test an invariant line: take a line and check that a point on it lands back on the same line. The eigenvectors hand you exactly these directions.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: Eigenvectors give the invariant lines of a transformation, since A·v = λv means v's direction is preserved. Powers are found via diagonalisation: Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹, where Dⁿ raises each eigenvalue to the power n.
例题Example
设 A = [2 1; 0 3]。求 A³。Let A = [2 1; 0 3]. Find A³.
① 对角化：P = [1 1; 0 1]，D = [2 0; 0 3]，P⁻¹ = [1 −1; 0 1]。① diagonalise: P = [1 1; 0 1], D = [2 0; 0 3], P⁻¹ = [1 −1; 0 1]. ② D³ = [8 0; 0 27]。② D³ = [8 0; 0 27]. ③ A³ = PD³P⁻¹ = [1 1; 0 1][8 0; 0 27][1 −1; 0 1]。③ A³ = PD³P⁻¹ = [1 1; 0 1][8 0; 0 27][1 −1; 0 1]. ④ = [8 27; 0 27][1 −1; 0 1] = [8, −8+27; 0, 27] = [8 19; 0 27]。④ = [8 27; 0 27][1 −1; 0 1] = [8, −8+27; 0, 27] = [8 19; 0 27].
练习Practice
先算 D⁴ = [2⁴ 0; 0 3⁴]，再夹乘出来。Find D⁴ = [2⁴ 0; 0 3⁴] first, then sandwich it out.

设 A = [2 1; 0 3]。用对角化求 A⁴。Let A = [2 1; 0 3]. Use diagonalisation to find A⁴.

点开看答案Show answer
D⁴ = [16 0; 0 81]。
A⁴ = PD⁴P⁻¹ = [1 1; 0 1][16 0; 0 81][1 −1; 0 1] = [16 81; 0 81][1 −1; 0 1] = [16 65; 0 81]。 D⁴ = [16 0; 0 81].
A⁴ = PD⁴P⁻¹ = [1 1; 0 1][16 0; 0 81][1 −1; 0 1] = [16 81; 0 81][1 −1; 0 1] = [16 65; 0 81].
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —

单元 9 小结Unit 9 summary

① 定义：A·v = λv——λ 是特征值、v 是特征向量，方向不变只被拉长 λ 倍。
② 求特征值：解特征方程 det(A − λI) = 0（2×2 是 λ 的二次方程）。
③ 求特征向量：λ 代回解齐次方程 (A−λI)v = 0，可差一个常数倍。
④ 对角化：A = PDP⁻¹，于是 Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹，Dⁿ 对角线各取 n 次方。
⑤ 应用：特征向量方向 = 不变线；对角化用来快速算矩阵的高次幂。① definition: A·v = λv — λ the eigenvalue, v the eigenvector; same direction, length × λ.
② finding eigenvalues: solve det(A − λI) = 0 (a quadratic in λ for 2×2).
③ finding eigenvectors: substitute λ and solve (A−λI)v = 0, up to a constant multiple.
④ diagonalisation: A = PDP⁻¹, so Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹, Dⁿ raises each diagonal entry to the n.
⑤ applications: eigenvector directions are invariant lines; diagonalisation gives fast matrix powers.

下一站：把微分再往深挖一层——微分进阶，学反三角/反双曲的导数、隐函数求导和参数方程求导。Next: dig one level deeper into calculus — Differentiation, with derivatives of inverse trig/hyperbolic functions, implicit differentiation and parametric differentiation.

单元 10 · 微分进阶：不给「y = …」也能求导Unit 10 · Further differentiation

CAIE 9231 Paper 2 · 5 站 · 隐函数、参数方程、双曲函数、二阶导CAIE 9231 Paper 2 · 5 stops · implicit, parametric, hyperbolic and second derivatives

路线图本单元五站，先搞懂「隐函数、参数方程」这两种「不给现成 y=…」的求导方法，再学双曲函数求导，最后上二阶导，并用切线、极值、相关变化率收尾Five stops — master implicit and parametric differentiation (where no ready-made y = … is given), then differentiate hyperbolic functions, then second derivatives, and close with tangents, extrema and related rates

  1. 隐函数求导：方程里 x、y 缠在一起Implicit differentiation
  2. 参数方程求导：x、y 都由 t 驱动Parametric differentiation
  3. 双曲函数求导：sinh、cosh、tanhHyperbolic derivatives
  4. 二阶导与二阶微分：凹凸性Second derivatives and concavity
  5. 综合应用：切线、极值、相关变化率Applied problems

建议：第 1 站的「链式法则 + 把 y 看成 x 的函数」是后面所有站的总开关，先把隐函数搞透，参数方程和二阶导都是它的顺水推舟。Tip: stop 1's "chain rule + treating y as a function of x" is the master switch for everything after — master implicit first, and parametric and second derivatives follow naturally.

1 隐函数求导：方程里 x、y 缠在一起Implicit differentiation implicit differentiation隐函数求导
这一站要你会：对 x、y 混写的方程两边求导，把 dy/dx 单独解出来，并会代入具体点算切线斜率。Goal: differentiate both sides of an equation mixing x and y, solve for dy/dx, and evaluate it at a given point for the tangent slope.
以前求导，你拿到的都是「y = 什么」这种现成函数。可圆、椭圆这类方程，x 和 y 缠在一起，解不出「干净的 y」怎么办？办法就是不拆开，直接对整个方程两边求导——把 y 当成 x 的函数，凡碰到 y 就用链式法则补一个 dy/dx，求完再移项，把 dy/dx 单独拎出来。这就是隐函数求导，「隐」就隐在 y 藏在方程里、没亮出来。Until now you were handed ready-made "y = …" functions. But for circles and ellipses, x and y are tangled and you can't solve a clean y. The trick: don't untangle — differentiate the whole equation on both sides, treating y as a function of x. Every y picks up a dy/dx by the chain rule; then rearrange to isolate dy/dx. That's implicit differentiation — "implicit" because y is hidden inside the equation.
  • 规则：对 x 求导照常；对 y 求导后要补乘 dy/dx（链式法则）。Rule: differentiate x terms as usual; every y term gains a dy/dx (chain rule).
  • 目标：求完后移项，把 dy/dx 单独解出来（答案通常同时含 x 和 y）。Aim: rearrange to isolate dy/dx (the answer usually still contains both x and y).
  • 记忆锚点：y² 对 x 求导 = 2y·(dy/dx)，不是 2y。Anchor: d/dx(y²) = 2y·(dy/dx), not 2y.
常见丢分：忘了「y² 求导要补 dy/dx」，直接写 2y 就错了；或者把 y 当成常数。记住：y 是 x 的函数，不是常数。Classic slip: forgetting the dy/dx on y² (writing just 2y), or treating y as a constant. Remember: y is a function of x, not a constant.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: Differentiate both sides of the equation with respect to x, treating y as a function of x. By the chain rule, d/dx(y²) = 2y dy/dx. Then rearrange to solve for dy/dx.
例题Example
求 x² + y² = 25 在点 (3, 4) 处的 dy/dx。Find dy/dx for x² + y² = 25 at the point (3, 4).
① 两边对 x 求导：2x + 2y·(dy/dx) = 0。① differentiate both sides w.r.t. x: 2x + 2y·(dy/dx) = 0. ② 移项：2y·(dy/dx) = −2x → dy/dx = −x/y。② rearrange: 2y·(dy/dx) = −2x → dy/dx = −x/y. ③ 代入 (3, 4)：dy/dx = −3/4。③ substitute (3, 4): dy/dx = −3/4.
练习Practice
xy 是乘积，要用乘法法则：d/dx(xy) = y + x·(dy/dx)。xy is a product — use the product rule: d/dx(xy) = y + x·(dy/dx).

已知 x² + xy + y² = 1，求 dy/dx（用 x、y 表示）。Given x² + xy + y² = 1, find dy/dx in terms of x and y.

点开看答案Show answer
求导：2x + (y + x·dy/dx) + 2y·dy/dx = 0 → (x + 2y)·dy/dx = −(2x + y) → dy/dx = −(2x+y)/(x+2y)。 Differentiate: 2x + (y + x·dy/dx) + 2y·dy/dx = 0 → (x + 2y)·dy/dx = −(2x + y) → dy/dx = −(2x+y)/(x+2y).
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 参数方程求导：x、y 都由 t 驱动Parametric differentiation parametric differentiation参数方程求导
这一站要你会：记住 dy/dx = (dy/dt)/(dx/dt)，会各自对 t 求导再相除，能求参数曲线上一点的切线斜率。Goal: recall dy/dx = (dy/dt)/(dx/dt), differentiate each w.r.t. t and divide, and find the tangent slope at a point on a parametric curve.
参数方程把 x、y 都写成 t 的函数，像用同一个「时间 t」驱动两个坐标。想求 dy/dx 怎么办？先各自对 t 求导，再把 dy/dt 除以 dx/dt。为什么？因为 dy/dx = (dy/dt)·(dt/dx)，而 dt/dx 正好是 dx/dt 的倒数。一句话记：「y 对 t 求导」除以「x 对 t 求导」。别去「直接对 t 求 dy/dx」，那是没意义的东西。Parametrics write both x and y as functions of t — like one "time t" driving two coordinates. To find dy/dx, differentiate each w.r.t. t, then divide dy/dt by dx/dt. Why? Because dy/dx = (dy/dt)·(dt/dx), and dt/dx is exactly the reciprocal of dx/dt. In one line: "d/dt of y" divided by "d/dt of x". Never try to take dy/dx "with respect to t" directly — that's meaningless.
dy/dx = (dy/dt) / (dx/dt) 前提：dx/dt ≠ 0
  • 步骤：① 求 dy/dt；② 求 dx/dt；③ 相除化简。Steps: ① find dy/dt; ② find dx/dt; ③ divide and simplify.
  • 为什么对：dy/dx = (dy/dt)·(dt/dx)，而 dt/dx = 1/(dx/dt)。Why it works: dy/dx = (dy/dt)·(dt/dx), and dt/dx = 1/(dx/dt).
  • 注意：dx/dt = 0 时切线竖直，dy/dx 无定义。Watch: when dx/dt = 0 the tangent is vertical and dy/dx is undefined.
常见丢分：把 dy/dx 和 dx/dt 搞反（把分母写成 dx/dt 该在上面的位置）。列式时先写清「上面是 dy/dt、下面是 dx/dt」，再代入。Classic slip: swapping dy/dt and dx/dt. Before substituting, write clearly "top = dy/dt, bottom = dx/dt".
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: If x = f(t) and y = g(t), then dy/dx = (dy/dt)/(dx/dt), by the chain rule, provided dx/dt ≠ 0.
例题Example
x = cos t、y = sin t，求 dy/dx（用 t 表示），并求 t = π/4 时的切线斜率。Given x = cos t and y = sin t, find dy/dx in terms of t, and the tangent slope at t = π/4.
① dy/dt = cos t，dx/dt = −sin t。① dy/dt = cos t, dx/dt = −sin t. ② dy/dx = (cos t)/(−sin t) = −cot t。② dy/dx = (cos t)/(−sin t) = −cot t. ③ t = π/4 时 cot(π/4) = 1，所以切线斜率 = −1。③ at t = π/4, cot(π/4) = 1, so the tangent slope = −1.
练习Practice
先求 dy/dt = 3t²、dx/dt = 2t，再相除。Find dy/dt = 3t² and dx/dt = 2t, then divide.

x = t²、y = t³，求 dy/dx（用 t 表示）。Given x = t² and y = t³, find dy/dx in terms of t.

点开看答案Show answer
dy/dt = 3t²，dx/dt = 2t。
dy/dx = (3t²)/(2t) = (3/2)t（t ≠ 0）。 dy/dt = 3t², dx/dt = 2t.
dy/dx = (3t²)/(2t) = (3/2)t (t ≠ 0).
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 双曲函数求导：sinh、cosh、tanhHyperbolic derivatives hyperbolic derivatives双曲函数求导
这一站要你会：记住三个基本公式，会从 e 指数定义自己推导，会用链式法则处理 sinh(ax)、cosh(ax) 这种复合形式。Goal: recall the three basic formulas, derive them from the exponential definitions, and use the chain rule for composites like sinh(ax) and cosh(ax).
双曲函数的求导比三角函数还省心——来回一对、没有负号：sinh 求导变 cosh，cosh 求导变 sinh（不是 −sinh！），tanh 求导变 sech²。对比一下三角函数你会记得更牢：cos 求导是 −sin 带负号，cosh 求导是 sinh 不带。遇到 sinh(2x) 这类复合的，链式法则补一个内层导数就行。Hyperbolic derivatives are even tidier than trig — a back-and-forth pair with no minus sign: sinh goes to cosh, cosh goes to sinh (not −sinh!), tanh goes to sech². Comparing with trig makes it stick: the derivative of cos is −sin (with a minus), while the derivative of cosh is sinh (no minus). For composites like sinh(2x), just multiply by the inner derivative by the chain rule.
d/dx sinh x = cosh x d/dx cosh x = sinh x d/dx tanh x = sech² x
  • 复合：d/dx sinh(ax) = a·cosh(ax)，d/dx cosh(ax) = a·sinh(ax)。Composites: d/dx sinh(ax) = a·cosh(ax), d/dx cosh(ax) = a·sinh(ax).
  • 由 e 指数推导：sinh x = (eˣ−e⁻ˣ)/2，求导得 cosh x。From exponentials: sinh x = (eˣ−e⁻ˣ)/2 differentiates to cosh x.
记忆钩子：三角里 cos 求导「掉个负号」，双曲里 cosh 求导「不掉」；就这一个区别，其余照抄。tanh 的 sech² 也别写成 −sech²。Memory hook: in trig, cos drops a minus; in hyperbolics, cosh doesn't. That's the only difference — everything else copies over. And don't write −sech² for tanh.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The derivatives of the hyperbolic functions are d/dx sinh x = cosh x, d/dx cosh x = sinh x, and d/dx tanh x = sech²x. Note there is no minus sign in d/dx cosh x.
例题Example
求 d/dx [sinh(3x) + cosh²x]。Find d/dx [sinh(3x) + cosh²x].
① d/dx sinh(3x) = 3 cosh(3x)（链式法则）。① d/dx sinh(3x) = 3 cosh(3x) (chain rule). ② d/dx cosh²x = 2 cosh x · (d/dx cosh x) = 2 cosh x · sinh x。② d/dx cosh²x = 2 cosh x · (d/dx cosh x) = 2 cosh x · sinh x. ③ 合并：3 cosh(3x) + 2 cosh x sinh x。③ combine: 3 cosh(3x) + 2 cosh x sinh x.
练习Practice
外层 tanh 的导数是 sech²，内层 2x 的导数是 2。The outer tanh gives sech², the inner 2x gives 2.

求 d/dx tanh(2x)。Find d/dx tanh(2x).

点开看答案Show answer
d/dx tanh(2x) = sech²(2x) · 2 = 2 sech²(2x)。 d/dx tanh(2x) = sech²(2x) · 2 = 2 sech²(2x).
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 二阶导与二阶微分：凹凸性Second derivatives and concavity second derivatives二阶导
这一站要你会：会算 d²y/dx²（显式与参数两种），能用 f″ 的正负判断曲线的凹凸性与拐点。Goal: compute d²y/dx² (both explicit and parametric forms), and use the sign of f″ to judge concavity and locate inflections.
一阶导是「速度」，二阶导是「加速度」——它告诉你曲线往哪个方向弯。f″ 是正，曲线像碗一样往上兜（上弯）；f″ 是负，像拱桥一样往下罩（下弯）；f″=0 的地方是「拐点」，曲线在这里换方向。参数方程求二阶导最坑：不是简单把 dy/dx 对 t 求导就完事，还要再除以 dx/dt。很多人就栽在「忘了再除一次」。The first derivative is "speed"; the second is "acceleration" — it tells you which way the curve bends. If f″ is positive the curve cups upward (like a bowl); if negative it arches downward (like a bridge); where f″=0 is an "inflection", the curve changes direction. Parametric second derivatives are the trickiest: it's not enough to differentiate dy/dx w.r.t. t — you must also divide by dx/dt. Many lose marks by forgetting that second division.
d²y/dx² = (d/dx)(dy/dx) d²y/dx² = (d(dy/dx)/dt) / (dx/dt)
  • 定义：d²y/dx² 是 dy/dx 再对 x 求一次导。Definition: d²y/dx² is the derivative of dy/dx w.r.t. x.
  • 参数二阶导：先把 dy/dx 对 t 求导，再除以 dx/dt。Parametric second derivative: differentiate dy/dx w.r.t. t, then divide by dx/dt.
  • 凹凸：f″>0 上弯（∪）；f″<0 下弯（∩）；f″=0 可能是拐点。Concavity: f″>0 concave up (∪); f″<0 concave down (∩); f″=0 may be an inflection.
参数二阶导的「再除一次 dx/dt」是最常见失分点。步骤：① 先求 dy/dx（结果仍是 t 的函数）→ ② 把 dy/dx 对 t 求导 → ③ 再除以 dx/dt。三步一步都别省。Forgetting the second dx/dt division is the classic slip. Steps: ① find dy/dx (still a function of t) → ② differentiate dy/dx w.r.t. t → ③ divide by dx/dt. Don't skip any of the three.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The second derivative d²y/dx² is d/dx(dy/dx). For parametrics, d²y/dx² = (d/dt(dy/dx))/(dx/dt). The sign of f″ tells the concavity: f″ > 0 concave up, f″ < 0 concave down.
例题Example
x = cos t、y = sin t，求 d²y/dx²（用 t 表示）。Given x = cos t and y = sin t, find d²y/dx² in terms of t.
① dy/dx = (cos t)/(−sin t) = −cot t。① dy/dx = (cos t)/(−sin t) = −cot t. ② d(dy/dx)/dt = d/dt(−cot t) = csc² t。② d(dy/dx)/dt = d/dt(−cot t) = csc² t. ③ dx/dt = −sin t，所以 d²y/dx² = csc²t / (−sin t) = −csc³ t。③ dx/dt = −sin t, so d²y/dx² = csc²t / (−sin t) = −csc³ t.
练习Practice
先求 dy/dx = (3/2)t，再对 t 求导得 3/2，最后除以 dx/dt = 2t。First dy/dx = (3/2)t, then d/dt gives 3/2, finally divide by dx/dt = 2t.

x = t²、y = t³，求 d²y/dx²。Given x = t² and y = t³, find d²y/dx².

点开看答案Show answer
dy/dx = (3/2)t → 对 t 求导 = 3/2 → 除以 dx/dt = 2t。
d²y/dx² = (3/2)/(2t) = 3/(4t)。 dy/dx = (3/2)t → d/dt = 3/2 → divide by dx/dt = 2t.
d²y/dx² = (3/2)/(2t) = 3/(4t).
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 综合应用：切线、极值、相关变化率Applied problems applied differentiation综合应用
这一站要你会：用 dy/dx 写切线、法线方程，求极大极小值（用 f′ 与 f″ 判断），并解相关变化率问题。Goal: write tangent and normal equations, find maxima/minima (using f′ and f″), and solve related-rates problems.
前面四站是「零件」，这一站是「组装」：切线就是「过点 + 斜率 dy/dx」套点斜式，法线是切线的垂直版本（斜率取负倒数）；极值先令 f′=0 找到候选点，再用 f″ 判断是极大还是极小；相关变化率里两个量都随时间 t 变，两边对 t 求导、用链式法则把它们的速度串起来。会组装，应用题就是送分题。The first four stops were the parts; this stop is assembly. A tangent is "point + slope dy/dx" in point-slope form; the normal is its perpendicular twin (slope is the negative reciprocal). For extrema, set f′=0 to find candidates, then use f″ to tell max from min. In related rates, two quantities change with time t — differentiate both sides w.r.t. t and the chain rule links their speeds. Master the assembly and these questions give away marks.
  • 切线：y − y₁ = m(x − x₁)，其中 m = dy/dx 在该点取值。Tangent: y − y₁ = m(x − x₁), with m = dy/dx evaluated there.
  • 法线：斜率 = −1/m，方程 y − y₁ = (−1/m)(x − x₁)。Normal: slope = −1/m, equation y − y₁ = (−1/m)(x − x₁).
  • 极值：f′(x)=0 得驻点，f″>0 极小、f″<0 极大。Extrema: f′(x)=0 gives stationary points; f″>0 minimum, f″<0 maximum.
  • 相关变化率：对时间 t 求导，链式法则连接各变化率。Related rates: differentiate w.r.t. time t; the chain rule links the rates.
相关变化率的套路：① 写清两个量之间的几何关系式（面积、体积、勾股…）→ ② 两边对 t 求导 → ③ 代入已知变化率和数值，解出未知变化率。先求导、后代数，别反了。Related-rates recipe: ① write the geometric relation between the two quantities (area, volume, Pythagoras…) → ② differentiate both sides w.r.t. t → ③ substitute known rates and values, solve for the unknown rate. Differentiate first, substitute last.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The tangent at (x₁, y₁) has equation y − y₁ = m(x − x₁) with m = dy/dx; the normal has slope −1/m. For extrema, solve f′(x) = 0 and test the sign of f″. For related rates, differentiate the linking equation with respect to time t.
例题Example
曲线 y = x² 在点 (1, 1) 处的切线和法线方程各是什么？Find the tangent and normal to y = x² at the point (1, 1).
① dy/dx = 2x，在 x = 1 处 m = 2。① dy/dx = 2x, so at x = 1, m = 2. ② 切线：y − 1 = 2(x − 1) → y = 2x − 1。② tangent: y − 1 = 2(x − 1) → y = 2x − 1. ③ 法线斜率 = −1/2：y − 1 = (−1/2)(x − 1) → 2y + x = 3。③ normal slope = −1/2: y − 1 = (−1/2)(x − 1) → 2y + x = 3.
练习Practice
先求 dy/dx = 3x²，在 x=1 处 m=3，点为 (1,1)。First dy/dx = 3x², so m = 3 at x = 1, at the point (1,1).

求曲线 y = x³ 在 x = 1 处的切线方程。Find the tangent to y = x³ at x = 1.

点开看答案Show answer
点 (1,1)，m = 3x²|_{x=1} = 3。
切线：y − 1 = 3(x − 1) → y = 3x − 2。 Point (1,1), m = 3x² at x=1 = 3.
Tangent: y − 1 = 3(x − 1) → y = 3x − 2.
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —

单元 10 小结Unit 10 summary

① 隐函数：两边对 x 求导，y 每次出现补 dy/dx（链式），再解出 dy/dx。
② 参数方程：dy/dx = (dy/dt)/(dx/dt)，二阶导再除一次 dx/dt。
③ 双曲函数：d/dx sinh = cosh、cosh = sinh、tanh = sech²，cosh 求导无负号。
④ 二阶导判凹凸：f″>0 上弯、f″<0 下弯、f″=0 拐点。
⑤ 应用：切线点斜式、法线负倒数斜率、极值令 f′=0、相关变化率对 t 求导。① implicit: differentiate both sides w.r.t. x, each y picks up dy/dx, then solve for dy/dx.
② parametric: dy/dx = (dy/dt)/(dx/dt); for the second derivative divide by dx/dt again.
③ hyperbolic: d/dx sinh = cosh, cosh = sinh, tanh = sech² — no minus for cosh.
④ concavity: f″>0 up, f″<0 down, f″=0 inflection.
⑤ applied: tangent point-slope, normal slope −1/m, extrema f′=0, related rates differentiate w.r.t. t.

下一站：积分进阶——把微分的工具反着用，学有理函数、换元、分部积分等进阶技巧。Next: integration — run differentiation in reverse, with rational functions, substitution and integration by parts.

单元 11 · 积分进阶：把「求导的反问题」玩出花Unit 11 · Further integration

CAIE 9231 Paper 2 · 6 站 · 换元、分部、递推，以及弧长和旋转体CAIE 9231 Paper 2 · 6 stops · substitution, parts, reduction, arc length and volumes of revolution

路线图本单元六站，先学三把「积分工具」——换元、分部、递推，再攻双曲函数积分，最后用积分去量弧长和旋转体的体积、侧面积，收尾做综合题Six stops — three integration tools (substitution, parts, reduction), then hyperbolic integrals, then arc length and surfaces of revolution, ending with combined problems

  1. 换元积分法：让复合函数显形Integration by substitution
  2. 分部积分法：乘积怎么积Integration by parts
  3. 递推公式：一族积分串起来Reduction formulae
  4. 双曲函数积分Integrating hyperbolic functions
  5. 弧长与旋转体Arc length and surfaces of revolution
  6. 综合应用：多招并用Applied problems

建议：前三站是「三件套」基本功，尤其换元和分部要练到闭眼会选。递推、双曲都是它们的「特化版本」，弧长和旋转体只是「切片求和」思路的换皮。Tip: the first three stops are the core toolkit — drill substitution and parts until they are automatic. Reduction and hyperbolic integrals are special cases, and arc length / volumes are just the "slice and sum" idea in disguise.

1 换元积分法：让复合函数显形Integration by substitution u-substitutionu 替换
这一站要你会：认出该用换元的积分（被积函数里藏着「一个函数和它的导数」），做 u 替换，定积分时把积分限也换成 u 的值。Goal: spot an integral worth substituting (a function sitting next to its derivative), carry out the u-substitution, and change the limits for definite integrals.
换元积分就像替身术。碰到 ∫2x(x²+1)³ dx 这种式子，x²+1 是个「盒子」，前面的 2x 恰好是盒子的导数——这时把整个盒子拎出来当新变量：令 u = x²+1，du = 2x dx，原积分一下缩水成 ∫u³ du，积完再把 u 换回 x²+1。关键就一句：内层函数和它的导数要成对出现。定积分还要记得把上下限一起换成 u 的值，省得积完再代回。Substitution is a body-swap trick. In ∫2x(x²+1)³ dx the x²+1 is a "box" and the 2x is exactly the box's derivative — so lift the whole box out as a new variable: let u = x²+1, du = 2x dx, and the integral shrinks to ∫u³ du; integrate, then put x²+1 back. The one rule: the inner function and its derivative must appear together. For definite integrals, swap the limits to u-values too, so you never substitute back.
  • 核心换元：令 u = g(x)，du = g′(x)dx，则 ∫ f(g(x))g′(x)dx = ∫ f(u)du。Core: let u = g(x), du = g′(x)dx, then ∫ f(g(x))g′(x)dx = ∫ f(u)du.
  • 三角替换：√(a²−x²) 令 x = a sinθ；√(a²+x²) 令 x = a tanθ。Trig substitution: √(a²−x²) use x = a sinθ; √(a²+x²) use x = a tanθ.
  • 基本积分别忘：∫(1/x)dx = ln|x| + C。Recall: ∫(1/x)dx = ln|x| + C.
常见丢分：定积分换元忘了换限，或只换了 u 没换 dx（漏掉 g′(x)）。每次换元都问自己两件事：du 写对了吗？积分限换了吗？Classic slips: forgetting to change the limits, or swapping u but not dx (dropping g′(x)). After every substitution ask: is du right? are the limits changed?
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: Let u = g(x), so du = g′(x) dx. Then ∫ f(g(x)) g′(x) dx = ∫ f(u) du, and for a definite integral the limits must be changed to the corresponding values of u.
例题Example
求 ∫ 2x(x²+1)³ dx。Find ∫ 2x(x²+1)³ dx.
① 令 u = x²+1，则 du = 2x dx。① let u = x²+1, so du = 2x dx. ② 原式 = ∫ u³ du = u⁴/4 + C。② integral = ∫ u³ du = u⁴/4 + C. ③ 代回 u = x²+1：(x²+1)⁴/4 + C。③ substitute back: (x²+1)⁴/4 + C.
练习Practice
内层是 x²，它的导数是 2x，只差一个常数 2。The inner part is x²; its derivative is 2x, just off by the constant 2.

求 ∫ x cos(x²) dx。Find ∫ x cos(x²) dx.

点开看答案Show answer
令 u = x²，du = 2x dx，所以 x dx = du/2。
∫ cos(u)·(du/2) = sin(u)/2 + C = sin(x²)/2 + C。 Let u = x², du = 2x dx, so x dx = du/2.
∫ cos(u)·(du/2) = sin(u)/2 + C = sin(x²)/2 + C.
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 分部积分法：乘积怎么积Integration by parts LIATE分部积分
这一站要你会：背下 ∫u dv = uv − ∫v du，用 LIATE 顺序挑 u，并能算出 x eˣ、x sin x 这类乘积的积分。Goal: recall ∫u dv = uv − ∫v du, choose u by LIATE, and integrate products like x eˣ and x sin x.
两个函数相乘怎么积分？没有现成的「乘法公式」，但可以用分部积分把球踢到另一边：∫u dv = uv − ∫v du。妙处在于——你可以挑谁当 u。挑得好，右边那个新积分比原来简单；挑反了，越积越乱。经验法则 LIATE：对数、反三角、代数、三角、指数，越靠前越优先当 u。为什么？因为对数和反三角没有简单的积分公式，干脆让它们当 u、被求导「消掉」。How do you integrate a product? There is no "product rule" for integrals, but integration by parts kicks the ball to the other side: ∫u dv = uv − ∫v du. The beauty is you choose who becomes u. Choose well and the new integral is simpler; choose badly and it gets messier. The rule of thumb LIATE: Logarithmic, Inverse trig, Algebraic, Trigonometric, Exponential — the further left, the more likely it should be u. Why? Logs and inverse trig lack simple integrals, so make them u and let differentiation "kill" them.
  • 公式：∫ u dv = uv − ∫ v du。Formula: ∫ u dv = uv − ∫ v du.
  • 选 u 的 LIATE 顺序：对数 → 反三角 → 代数 → 三角 → 指数。LIATE order for u: Logarithmic → Inverse trig → Algebraic → Trigonometric → Exponential.
  • 直觉：谁求导后变简单，谁就当 u。Intuition: make u the part that gets simpler when differentiated.
LIATE 是默认顺序，但有例外：如果 dv 很好积、而 u 求导后更简单，就大胆换。核心一句话——让右边的新积分更简单。LIATE is the default, not a law: if dv is easy to integrate and u gets simpler when differentiated, swap freely. The only goal: make the new integral simpler.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: Integration by parts: ∫ u dv = uv − ∫ v du. Choose u using LIATE (Logarithmic, Inverse trig, Algebraic, Trigonometric, Exponential).
例题Example
求 ∫ x eˣ dx。Find ∫ x eˣ dx.
① 选 u = x（代数排在指数前），dv = eˣ dx。① choose u = x (algebraic beats exponential), dv = eˣ dx. ② 则 du = dx，v = eˣ。② so du = dx, v = eˣ. ③ ∫x eˣ dx = x eˣ − ∫ eˣ dx = x eˣ − eˣ + C = eˣ(x − 1) + C。③ ∫x eˣ dx = x eˣ − ∫ eˣ dx = x eˣ − eˣ + C = eˣ(x − 1) + C.
练习Practice
u = x，dv = sin x dx。Take u = x, dv = sin x dx.

求 ∫ x sin x dx。Find ∫ x sin x dx.

点开看答案Show answer
u = x，dv = sin x dx → du = dx，v = −cos x。
∫x sin x dx = −x cos x + ∫ cos x dx = −x cos x + sin x + C。 u = x, dv = sin x dx → du = dx, v = −cos x.
∫x sin x dx = −x cos x + ∫ cos x dx = −x cos x + sin x + C.
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 递推公式：一族积分串起来Reduction formulae Iₙ → Iₙ₋₂递推
这一站要你会：对 Iₙ = ∫sinⁿx dx 用分部积分推出 Iₙ = ((n−1)/n)Iₙ₋₂，并用它把高阶幂的积分一路降到 I₀ 或 I₁。Goal: derive Iₙ = ((n−1)/n)Iₙ₋₂ for Iₙ = ∫sinⁿx dx by parts, then use it to reduce a high power down to I₀ or I₁.
递推公式是「打包处理一族积分」的聪明办法。∫sinⁿx dx 里的 n 可以是一、二、三……无穷多个积分，总不能一个个硬算。递推的思路是：用一次分部积分，把 Iₙ 和「低两阶」的 Iₙ₋₂ 挂上钩。这样 I₅ 用 I₃，I₃ 用 I₁，一路降下去，最后落到一个你知道的 I₀ 或 I₁ 上，问题就解开了。像多米诺骨牌，推倒第一个，后面全跟着倒。Reduction formulae are a clever way to "batch-process" a whole family of integrals. In ∫sinⁿx dx the n can be one, two, three… infinitely many integrals — you can't grind each one out. The idea: one round of integration by parts links Iₙ to Iₙ₋₂, two levels lower. Then I₅ uses I₃, I₃ uses I₁, all the way down to a known I₀ or I₁ — done. Like dominoes: knock the first and the rest fall.
对 Iₙ = ∫ sinⁿx dx，分部积分得到递推公式：For Iₙ = ∫ sinⁿx dx, integration by parts gives the reduction formula:
Iₙ = ((n−1)/n) · Iₙ₋₂
  • 定积分版（0 到 π/2 时，边界项为 0）：Iₙ = ((n−1)/n)·Iₙ₋₂。Definite form (on 0 to π/2, boundary term vanishes): Iₙ = ((n−1)/n)·Iₙ₋₂.
  • 常用结果：∫ sin²x dx = x/2 − sin 2x/4 + C。Useful result: ∫ sin²x dx = x/2 − sin 2x/4 + C.
cosⁿx 也适用：递推公式对 ∫cosⁿx dx 形式一模一样。只在 0 到 π/2 的定积分上最省事，因为边界项恰好为零。Works for cos too: the reduction formula for ∫cosⁿx dx has exactly the same form. It is cleanest on definite integrals 0 to π/2, where the boundary term vanishes.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: Let Iₙ = ∫ sinⁿx dx. Integrating by parts gives Iₙ = ((n−1)/n) Iₙ₋₂.
例题Example
用递推公式求 ∫₀^{π/2} sin⁴x dx。Use the reduction formula to find ∫₀^{π/2} sin⁴x dx.
① I₄ = (3/4)·I₂。① I₄ = (3/4)·I₂. ② I₂ = (1/2)·I₀，而 I₀ = ∫₀^{π/2} dx = π/2。② I₂ = (1/2)·I₀, with I₀ = ∫₀^{π/2} dx = π/2. ③ I₄ = (3/4)(1/2)(π/2) = 3π/16。③ I₄ = (3/4)(1/2)(π/2) = 3π/16.
练习Practice
I₅ = (4/5)I₃，I₃ = (2/3)I₁，I₁ = ∫₀^{π/2} sin x dx = 1。I₅ = (4/5)I₃, I₃ = (2/3)I₁, and I₁ = ∫₀^{π/2} sin x dx = 1.

求 ∫₀^{π/2} sin⁵x dx。Find ∫₀^{π/2} sin⁵x dx.

点开看答案Show answer
I₅ = (4/5)·I₃ = (4/5)(2/3)·I₁ = (8/15)·1 = 8/15。 I₅ = (4/5)·I₃ = (4/5)(2/3)·I₁ = (8/15)·1 = 8/15.
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 双曲函数积分：cosh 和 sinhIntegrating hyperbolic functions sinh ↔ cosh双曲
这一站要你会：记住 ∫sinh = cosh、∫cosh = sinh 及几个常用双曲积分，并用双曲替换 x = sinh u 处理 √(x²+1)。Goal: recall ∫sinh = cosh, ∫cosh = sinh and the common hyperbolic integrals, and use x = sinh u to handle √(x²+1).
双曲函数听着吓人，其实是一对「指数双胞胎」：sinh x = (eˣ−e⁻ˣ)/2，cosh x = (eˣ+e⁻ˣ)/2。最讨喜的是它俩互为导数：sinh 的导数是 cosh，cosh 的导数是 sinh，还不带负号。所以积分时也原样互换。碰上 √(x²+1) 这种根号，三角替换要用 tan，双曲替换 x = sinh u 更利落——因为 cosh²u − sinh²u = 1，根号瞬间变成 cosh u。Hyperbolic functions sound scary but are really "exponential twins": sinh x = (eˣ−e⁻ˣ)/2, cosh x = (eˣ+e⁻ˣ)/2. The nicest bit is that they are each other's derivative: d/dx sinh = cosh, d/dx cosh = sinh, with no minus sign. So integration swaps them in place. For a root like √(x²+1), the trig route uses tan, but the hyperbolic substitution x = sinh u is cleaner — because cosh²u − sinh²u = 1, the root instantly becomes cosh u.
  • 基础：∫ sinh x dx = cosh x + C；∫ cosh x dx = sinh x + C。Basics: ∫ sinh x dx = cosh x + C; ∫ cosh x dx = sinh x + C.
  • 进阶：∫ sech²x dx = tanh x + C；∫ tanh x dx = ln(cosh x) + C。More: ∫ sech²x dx = tanh x + C; ∫ tanh x dx = ln(cosh x) + C.
  • 双曲换元：√(x²+1) 令 x = sinh u；√(x²−1) 令 x = cosh u。Hyperbolic substitution: √(x²+1) use x = sinh u; √(x²−1) use x = cosh u.
别和三角搞混：三角求导会变号（cos 的导数是 −sin），双曲求导不变号（cosh 的导数是 sinh）。记住「双曲没有负号」这个反差。Don't confuse with trig: trig derivatives flip sign (cos differentiates to −sin), hyperbolic ones don't (cosh differentiates to sinh). Remember the contrast: "hyperbolic has no minus".
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: ∫ sinh x dx = cosh x + C and ∫ cosh x dx = sinh x + C. For √(x²+1), use the substitution x = sinh u.
例题Example
求 ∫ 1/√(x²+1) dx。Find ∫ 1/√(x²+1) dx.
① 令 x = sinh u，则 dx = cosh u du。① let x = sinh u, so dx = cosh u du. ② √(x²+1) = √(sinh²u + 1) = cosh u。② √(x²+1) = √(sinh²u + 1) = cosh u. ③ ∫ (cosh u / cosh u) du = ∫ du = u + C = arsinh x + C。③ ∫ (cosh u / cosh u) du = ∫ du = u + C = arsinh x + C.
练习Practice
线性替换：令 u = 3x。Linear substitution: let u = 3x.

求 ∫ cosh 3x dx。Find ∫ cosh 3x dx.

点开看答案Show answer
令 u = 3x，du = 3 dx，dx = du/3。
∫ cosh 3x dx = (1/3)∫ cosh u du = (1/3)sinh 3x + C。 Let u = 3x, du = 3 dx, dx = du/3.
∫ cosh 3x dx = (1/3)∫ cosh u du = (1/3)sinh 3x + C.
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 弧长与旋转体：积分去量长度和体积Arc length and surfaces of revolution s, V, S弧长·体积
这一站要你会：写出弧长、旋转体体积、旋转体侧面积三个公式，并会代入求值。Goal: write the three formulas — arc length, volume and surface area of revolution — and evaluate them.
积分不只能量「曲线下的面积」，还能量「曲线的长度」和「旋转体的体积」。想象把一根软尺贴在曲线上，每一小段长 √(1+(dy/dx)²)dx，加总就是弧长。再把曲线绕 x 轴转一圈，就转出一个花瓶形状的立体：体积是把每个薄圆盘 πy²dx 加起来，侧面积是把每个薄圆环 2πy√(1+(dy/dx)²)dx 加起来。三个公式共用同一招：切成无穷薄片再求和。Integration does more than measure area under a curve — it measures the curve's length and the volume of a solid of revolution. Imagine laying a tape measure along the curve: each tiny piece is √(1+(dy/dx)²)dx, and summing them gives the arc length. Spin the curve around the x-axis and you sweep out a vase-shaped solid: the volume adds up thin discs πy²dx, and the surface area adds up thin bands 2πy√(1+(dy/dx)²)dx. All three share one idea: slice into infinitely thin pieces and add.
曲线 y = f(x) 绕 x 轴转，从 a 到 b（s = 弧长，V = 体积，S = 侧面积）：For y = f(x) rotated about the x-axis, from a to b (s = arc length, V = volume, S = surface area):
s = ∫ₐᵇ √(1 + (dy/dx)²) dx V = π ∫ₐᵇ y² dx S = 2π ∫ₐᵇ y √(1 + (dy/dx)²) dx
  • 绕 y 轴：把 x、y 互换，dx 换成 dy。About the y-axis: swap x and y, and dx becomes dy.
侧面积最容易被漏：它比体积多一个 √(1+(dy/dx)²)，因为它量的是「斜着」的表面积，不是圆柱的直侧面。Surface area is the easiest to forget: it carries an extra √(1+(dy/dx)²) because it measures the slanted surface, not a straight cylinder wall.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: Arc length s = ∫ √(1+(dy/dx)²) dx. Volume of revolution V = π∫ y² dx. Surface area S = 2π∫ y√(1+(dy/dx)²) dx.
例题Example
求曲线 y = x² 从 x = 0 到 x = 1 绕 x 轴旋转所得立体的体积。Find the volume generated when y = x², from x = 0 to x = 1, is rotated about the x-axis.
① V = π ∫₀¹ (x²)² dx = π ∫₀¹ x⁴ dx。① V = π ∫₀¹ (x²)² dx = π ∫₀¹ x⁴ dx. ② = π [x⁵/5]₀¹ = π/5。② = π [x⁵/5]₀¹ = π/5.
练习Practice
y² = x，先代进公式再积。Here y² = x; substitute into the formula first.

求曲线 y = √x 从 x = 0 到 x = 4 绕 x 轴旋转所得立体的体积。Find the volume generated when y = √x, from x = 0 to x = 4, is rotated about the x-axis.

点开看答案Show answer
y² = x，所以 V = π ∫₀⁴ x dx = π [x²/2]₀⁴ = π·(16/2 − 0) = 8π。 y² = x, so V = π ∫₀⁴ x dx = π [x²/2]₀⁴ = π·(16/2 − 0) = 8π.
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 综合应用：多招并用Applied problems: combining techniques put it together综合
这一站要你会：面对一道「没贴标签」的积分题，自己判断用哪种技巧，并能组合分部积分与换元。Goal: face an unlabelled integral, decide which technique to use, and combine parts with substitution.
真考试不会贴标签告诉你「这题用分部」。它甩给你一个积分，你得自己相面：有乘积（x eˣ、x ln x）→ 想分部；函数套函数还带着内层导数 → 想换元；sinⁿx、cosⁿx 的幂 → 想递推；带 √(x²±1) → 想双曲换元。有些题还得组合出招：∫eˣ sin x dx 分部一次，右边又冒出原积分，那就再分部一次，把它当方程解出来。套路练熟，一眼定招。A real exam never labels a question "use parts". It hands you an integral and you must read the shape: a product (x eˣ, x ln x) → parts; a function inside a function with its derivative → substitution; powers of sinⁿx or cosⁿx → reduction; √(x²±1) → hyperbolic substitution. Some need combined moves: ∫eˣ sin x dx — parts once and the original integral reappears, so do parts again and solve it like an equation. Drill until you can name the tool at a glance.
  • 看长相选招：乘积 → 分部；复合 → 换元；幂 → 递推；√(x²±1) → 双曲。Pick by shape: product → parts; composite → substitution; power → reduction; √(x²±1) → hyperbolic.
  • 循环积分（eˣ sin x、eˣ cos x）：分部两遍，再解方程。Looping integrals (eˣ sin x, eˣ cos x): parts twice, then solve.
  • 隐形乘积：∫ ln x dx 看成 1·ln x，令 u = ln x、dv = dx。Hidden product: write ∫ ln x dx as 1·ln x with u = ln x, dv = dx.
循环积分有口诀：∫eˣ sin x dx = (eˣ/2)(sin x − cos x)。同理 ∫eˣ cos x dx = (eˣ/2)(sin x + cos x)，背下来能省不少时间。Remember the loops: ∫eˣ sin x dx = (eˣ/2)(sin x − cos x) and ∫eˣ cos x dx = (eˣ/2)(sin x + cos x). Learning these saves real time.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: For ∫ eˣ sin x dx, integrate by parts twice and solve for the original integral.
例题Example
求 ∫ eˣ sin x dx。Find ∫ eˣ sin x dx.
① 令 u = sin x，dv = eˣ dx → du = cos x dx，v = eˣ。得 ∫eˣ sin x dx = eˣ sin x − ∫ eˣ cos x dx。① u = sin x, dv = eˣ dx → du = cos x dx, v = eˣ. So ∫eˣ sin x dx = eˣ sin x − ∫ eˣ cos x dx. ② 对 ∫eˣ cos x dx 再分部：u = cos x，dv = eˣ dx → = eˣ cos x + ∫ eˣ sin x dx。② parts again on ∫eˣ cos x dx: u = cos x, dv = eˣ dx → = eˣ cos x + ∫ eˣ sin x dx. ③ 代回：∫eˣ sin x dx = eˣ sin x − eˣ cos x − ∫ eˣ sin x dx。③ substitute back: ∫eˣ sin x dx = eˣ sin x − eˣ cos x − ∫ eˣ sin x dx. ④ 移项：2∫eˣ sin x dx = eˣ(sin x − cos x)，所以 ∫eˣ sin x dx = (eˣ/2)(sin x − cos x) + C。④ rearrange: 2∫eˣ sin x dx = eˣ(sin x − cos x), so ∫eˣ sin x dx = (eˣ/2)(sin x − cos x) + C.
练习Practice
u = ln x，dv = x dx。Take u = ln x, dv = x dx.

求 ∫ x ln x dx。Find ∫ x ln x dx.

点开看答案Show answer
u = ln x，dv = x dx → du = dx/x，v = x²/2。
∫x ln x dx = (x²/2)ln x − ∫ (x²/2)(1/x) dx = (x²/2)ln x − ∫ (x/2) dx = (x²/2)ln x − x²/4 + C。 u = ln x, dv = x dx → du = dx/x, v = x²/2.
∫x ln x dx = (x²/2)ln x − ∫ (x²/2)(1/x) dx = (x²/2)ln x − ∫ (x/2) dx = (x²/2)ln x − x²/4 + C.
— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —

单元 11 小结Unit 11 summary

① 换元：令 u = g(x)，让复合函数显形，定积分别忘换积分限。
② 分部：∫u dv = uv − ∫v du，用 LIATE（对数→反三角→代数→三角→指数）选 u。
③ 递推：Iₙ = ∫sinⁿx dx → Iₙ = ((n−1)/n)Iₙ₋₂，一路降到 I₀ 或 I₁。
④ 双曲：∫sinh = cosh、∫cosh = sinh，√(x²+1) 用 x = sinh u。
⑤ 弧长与旋转体：s = ∫√(1+(dy/dx)²)dx，V = π∫y²dx，S = 2π∫y√(1+(dy/dx)²)dx。① Substitution: let u = g(x) to expose the composite; change the limits for definite integrals.
② Parts: ∫u dv = uv − ∫v du, choose u by LIATE (Log → Inverse trig → Algebraic → Trig → Exponential).
③ Reduction: Iₙ = ∫sinⁿx dx → Iₙ = ((n−1)/n)Iₙ₋₂, down to I₀ or I₁.
④ Hyperbolic: ∫sinh = cosh, ∫cosh = sinh; use x = sinh u for √(x²+1).
⑤ Arc length and solids: s = ∫√(1+(dy/dx)²)dx, V = π∫y²dx, S = 2π∫y√(1+(dy/dx)²)dx.

下一站：进入复数——那些「平方是负数」的数，和它们在复平面上的几何。Next: enter the complex numbers — the numbers whose squares can be negative, and their geometry in the complex plane.

单元 12 · 复数：给「负数的平方根」一个家Unit 12 · Complex numbers: a home for the square root of a negative
