1 复数与 Argand 图Complex numbers and the Argand diagram z = x + iy复数=a+bi
这一站要你会：写出 z = x + iy、说清 i² = −1；在复平面上标出复数；求模 |z| = √(x²+y²) 与辐角 arg z；会写共轭 z*。Goal: write z = x + iy and state i² = −1; plot a complex number; find the modulus |z| = √(x²+y²) and argument arg z; write the conjugate z*.
你大概早就被教过：负数不能开平方。但数学家的回答是——那就造一个数来开。造出 i，规定 i² = −1，于是 √−1 就有意义了。有了 i，任何复数都能写成 z = x + iy：x 是实部（正常的数），y 是虚部（i 前面的数）。把它画到平面上的点 (x, y)，这张图就叫 Argand 图（复平面）。模 |z| 是「这个点离原点多远」，勾股定理 √(x²+y²) 一算就得；辐角 arg z 是「这条线往哪个方向指」，用反正切。就像定位一个点，要说清它离原点多远、朝哪个方向。You were probably told a negative can't have a square root. The mathematician's answer: invent a number so it can. Define i with i² = −1, and √−1 makes sense. With i, every complex number is z = x + iy: x is the real part (an ordinary number), y the imaginary part (the number in front of i). Plot it at (x, y) and you get the Argand diagram. The modulus |z| is how far the point is from the origin — Pythagoras gives √(x²+y²); the argument arg z is the direction of the line — use inverse tangent. Just like locating a point: say how far and in which direction.
z = x + iy |z| = √(x² + y²) z* = x − iy
  • i² = −1；x 叫实部，y 叫虚部。i² = −1; x is the real part, y the imaginary part.
  • 模 |z| 是到原点的距离，用勾股定理。Modulus |z| is the distance from the origin, by Pythagoras.
  • 辐角 arg z = arctan(y/x)，但要看象限定角。Argument arg z = arctan(y/x), but fix the quadrant.
  • 共轭 z* = x − iy：虚部变号，图像上就是关于实轴翻折。Conjugate z* = x − iy: flip the imaginary sign — a reflection in the real axis.
常见丢分：辐角不唯一——同一个方向加 360° 还是同一个角，考试常取 −180° 到 180° 之间的主值。算 arg z 前先看清点在第几象限，别直接 arctan 了事（第二、三象限要加减 180°）。Classic slip: the argument isn't unique — adding 360° gives the same direction; exams usually take the principal value in (−180°, 180°]. Check the quadrant before trusting arctan (add or subtract 180° in quadrants II and III).
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The complex number z = x + iy has real part x and imaginary part y, where i² = −1. Its modulus is |z| = √(x² + y²), the distance from the origin on the Argand diagram, and its conjugate is z* = x − iy.
例题Example
设 z = 3 + 4i。求 |z|、arg z，并写出共轭 z*。Let z = 3 + 4i. Find |z|, arg z and the conjugate z*.
① 模：|z| = √(3² + 4²) = √25 = 5。① modulus: |z| = √(3² + 4²) = √25 = 5. ② 辐角：arg z = arctan(4/3) ≈ 53.1°（第一象限）。② argument: arg z = arctan(4/3) ≈ 53.1° (first quadrant). ③ 共轭：z* = 3 − 4i。③ conjugate: z* = 3 − 4i.
练习Practice
5–12–13 是勾股数，模一眼就是 13；辐角在第四象限，是负角。5–12–13 is a Pythagorean triple, so the modulus is 13 at once; the argument is negative (fourth quadrant).

设 z = 5 − 12i。求 |z| 与 arg z（保留一位小数）。Let z = 5 − 12i. Find |z| and arg z (to 1 d.p.).

点开看答案Show answer
|z| = √(5² + 12²) = √169 = 13。
arg z = arctan(−12/5) ≈ −67.4°（第四象限）。 |z| = √(5² + 12²) = √169 = 13.
arg z = arctan(−12/5) ≈ −67.4° (fourth quadrant).
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 极形式Polar form r cis θ极形式
这一站要你会：在直角形式与极形式之间来回转换；记住 x = r cos θ、y = r sin θ，以及 r = √(x²+y²)、θ = arg z。Goal: convert between rectangular and polar form; recall x = r cos θ, y = r sin θ, and r = √(x²+y²), θ = arg z.
直角形式像「往东走 3 步、往北走 4 步」，极形式像「朝东北方向走 5 步」——同一个点，两种说法。极形式的好处是：乘法和乘方会变得特别简单，后面两站全靠它。换算口诀记牢：由直角求极——r 用勾股定理、θ 用反正切；由极求直角——x = r cos θ、y = r sin θ。注意 r 总取正（它是距离），θ 要看清在第几象限。Rectangular form says "walk 3 east, 4 north"; polar form says "walk 5 north-east" — same point, two descriptions. Polar form's payoff: multiplication and powers become easy, which the next two stops rely on. Learn the conversions: rectangular → polar — r by Pythagoras, θ by arctan; polar → rectangular — x = r cos θ, y = r sin θ. Keep r positive (it is a distance) and watch the quadrant for θ.
z = x + iy = r(cos θ + i sin θ) = r cis θ r = √(x² + y²) x = r cos θ, y = r sin θ
  • r 是模（≥ 0），θ 是辐角。r is the modulus (≥ 0), θ the argument.
  • cis θ 是 cos θ + i sin θ 的简写。cis θ is shorthand for cos θ + i sin θ.
辐角要挑对象限：算 θ = arctan(y/x) 时，计算器只给 −90° 到 90° 的答案。第二、三象限要手动加 180°，第四象限是负角。拿不准就画个图。Pick the right quadrant: arctan(y/x) only returns (−90°, 90°). Add 180° in quadrants II and III, and expect a negative angle in IV. Draw a sketch if unsure.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: A complex number z = x + iy can be written in polar form z = r(cos θ + i sin θ), where r = |z| = √(x² + y²) and θ = arg z. Equivalently x = r cos θ and y = r sin θ.
例题Example
把 z = 1 + i 写成极形式 r(cos θ + i sin θ)。Write z = 1 + i in polar form r(cos θ + i sin θ).
① r = √(1² + 1²) = √2。① r = √(1² + 1²) = √2. ② θ = arctan(1/1) = π/4（45°，第一象限）。② θ = arctan(1/1) = π/4 (45°, first quadrant). ③ z = √2 (cos π/4 + i sin π/4) = √2 cis(π/4)。③ z = √2 (cos π/4 + i sin π/4) = √2 cis(π/4).
练习Practice
3–4–5，r 一眼是 5；θ = arctan(4/3)。3–4–5: r is clearly 5; θ = arctan(4/3).

把 z = 3 + 4i 写成极形式（辐角用度，保留一位小数）。Write z = 3 + 4i in polar form (argument in degrees, 1 d.p.).

点开看答案Show answer
r = 5，θ ≈ 53.1°。
z = 5(cos 53.1° + i sin 53.1°) = 5 cis 53.1°。 r = 5, θ ≈ 53.1°.
z = 5(cos 53.1° + i sin 53.1°) = 5 cis 53.1°.
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 de Moivre 定理de Moivre's theorem de Moivre's theorem棣莫弗定理
这一站要你会：背下 (cos θ + i sin θ)ⁿ = cos nθ + i sin nθ，并用它求复数的幂；对 r cis θ 的 n 次方，结果是 rⁿ cis(nθ)。Goal: recall (cos θ + i sin θ)ⁿ = cos nθ + i sin nθ and use it to find powers; for r cis θ raised to the n, the result is rⁿ cis(nθ).
这是复数里最好用的一条定理，名字拗口，叫 de Moivre（棣莫弗），干的事却特简单：角度乘 n，模乘 n 次方。以前算 (cos θ + i sin θ)⁵ 要展开五次、合并一大串；现在直接写 cos 5θ + i sin 5θ。配上极形式 r cis θ，乘方就三步：r 变成 rⁿ，θ 变成 nθ，完事。这站练熟，后面求单位根、指数形式全都踩在它肩上。The most useful theorem in complex numbers, with a clumsy name: de Moivre's theorem. What it does is simple — multiply the angle by n, raise the modulus to the n. Instead of expanding (cos θ + i sin θ)⁵ and merging piles of terms, you just write cos 5θ + i sin 5θ. With polar form r cis θ, a power is three steps: r becomes rⁿ, θ becomes nθ, done. Master this and the roots of unity and the exponential form all stand on its shoulders.
(cos θ + i sin θ)ⁿ = cos nθ + i sin nθ [r(cos θ + i sin θ)]ⁿ = rⁿ (cos nθ + i sin nθ)
  • 对整数 n 成立（进阶数学还会把它推广到分数与负整数）。True for integer n (Further Maths extends it to fractions and negative integers).
  • n = 2 时就是二倍角公式的复数版，能反过来推 cos 2θ、sin 2θ。At n = 2 it is the complex double-angle formula; invert it to derive cos 2θ and sin 2θ.
别搞错：是「角度乘 n」，不是 cos 乘 n。很多同学把 cos nθ 写成 n cos θ，那是大错。另外用 de Moivre 前，先把 z 写成极形式（r 和 θ 都要有）。Don't mix it up: it is the angle that multiplies by n, not the cosine. Writing n cos θ instead of cos nθ is a serious error. Also, put z in polar form (both r and θ) before applying de Moivre.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: By de Moivre's theorem, (cos θ + i sin θ)ⁿ = cos nθ + i sin nθ. To find zⁿ, first write z in polar form z = r(cos θ + i sin θ); then zⁿ = rⁿ(cos nθ + i sin nθ).
例题Example
用 de Moivre 定理求 (1 + i)⁶，答案写成直角形式。Use de Moivre's theorem to find (1 + i)⁶ in rectangular form.
① 1 + i 的极形式：r = √2、θ = π/4 → 1 + i = √2 cis(π/4)。① polar form of 1 + i: r = √2, θ = π/4, so 1 + i = √2 cis(π/4). ② (1+i)⁶ = (√2)⁶ cis(6·π/4) = 8 cis(3π/2)。② (1+i)⁶ = (√2)⁶ cis(6·π/4) = 8 cis(3π/2). ③ cos(3π/2)=0、sin(3π/2)=−1 → = 8(0 − i) = −8i。③ cos(3π/2)=0, sin(3π/2)=−1, so = 8(0 − i) = −8i.
练习Practice
r = √2、θ = π/4；三次方就是 r³、θ × 3。r = √2, θ = π/4; cube it: r³ and θ × 3.

用 de Moivre 定理求 (1 + i)³，写成直角形式。Use de Moivre's theorem to find (1 + i)³ in rectangular form.

点开看答案Show answer
(1+i)³ = (√2)³ cis(3π/4) = 2√2 (−√2/2 + i·√2/2) = −2 + 2i。 (1+i)³ = (√2)³ cis(3π/4) = 2√2 (−√2/2 + i·√2/2) = −2 + 2i.
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 复数方程与根Solving equations and roots roots of unity单位根
这一站要你会：求 zⁿ = 1 的 n 个单位根；二次方程判别式小于 0 时，用求根公式写出共轭复根。Goal: find the n roots of zⁿ = 1; when a quadratic has negative discriminant, write its conjugate complex roots via the formula.
普通数学里 x² + 1 = 0 会说「无实根」；但复数世界里，n 次方程一定有 n 个根（代数基本定理）。解 z⁴ = 1，根是 1、i、−1、−i——正好在单位圆上「东南西北」四个点，均匀分布，像钟表刻度。这就是「单位根」：zⁿ = 1 的 n 个根永远均匀铺在单位圆上，从角度 0 出发，往后每次加 2π/n。二次方程判别式 Δ < 0 也别怕，套求根公式，√(负数) 用 i 写出来，两个复根一定互为共轭。In ordinary maths x² + 1 = 0 has "no real roots"; in the complex world an nth-degree equation always has n roots (the fundamental theorem of algebra). Solving z⁴ = 1 gives 1, i, −1, −i — four points at north, east, south, west on the unit circle, evenly spaced like a clock face. These are the roots of unity: zⁿ = 1 always has n roots spread evenly round the unit circle, starting at angle 0 and adding 2π/n each time. Don't fear a quadratic with Δ < 0 — use the formula, write √(negative) with i, and the two complex roots are conjugates.
zⁿ = 1 → z = cos(2πk/n) + i sin(2πk/n) k = 0, 1, 2, …, n − 1
  • n 个单位根在单位圆上均匀分布，相邻相差 2π/n。The n roots sit evenly on the unit circle, 2π/n apart.
  • 二次方程 Δ<0：x = (−b ± i√(4ac−b²)) / 2a，两根互为共轭。Quadratic with Δ<0: x = (−b ± i√(4ac−b²)) / 2a; the roots are conjugates.
单位根口诀：n 个根 = 把单位圆均分 n 份。n 是偶数时 ±1 一定在根里（z⁴=1 有 1 和 −1）；n 是奇数时只有 1，没有 −1。Roots-of-unity shortcut: n roots = the unit circle cut into n equal parts. For even n, ±1 are always among them (z⁴=1 has 1 and −1); for odd n only 1 appears, never −1.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The equation zⁿ = 1 has exactly n roots, called the nth roots of unity. They are z = cos(2πk/n) + i sin(2πk/n) for k = 0, 1, …, n−1, evenly spaced on the unit circle.
例题Example
解方程 z⁴ = 1，写出全部四个根。Solve z⁴ = 1 and list all four roots.
① 把 1 写成极形式：1 = 1·cis(0)。① write 1 in polar form: 1 = 1·cis(0). ② 四根 = cis(2πk/4) = cis(kπ/2)，k = 0, 1, 2, 3。② the four roots are cis(2πk/4) = cis(kπ/2), k = 0, 1, 2, 3. ③ k=0 → cis 0 = 1；k=1 → cis(π/2) = i；k=2 → cis π = −1；k=3 → cis(3π/2) = −i。即 1, i, −1, −i。③ k=0 → cis 0 = 1; k=1 → cis(π/2) = i; k=2 → cis π = −1; k=3 → cis(3π/2) = −i, i.e. 1, i, −1, −i.
练习Practice
Δ = 4 − 20 = −16 < 0，√−16 = 4i。Δ = 4 − 20 = −16 < 0, and √−16 = 4i.

解方程 z² + 2z + 5 = 0。Solve z² + 2z + 5 = 0.

点开看答案Show answer
z = (−2 ± √(4 − 20)) / 2 = (−2 ± √−16) / 2 = (−2 ± 4i) / 2 = −1 ± 2i。 z = (−2 ± √(4 − 20)) / 2 = (−2 ± √−16) / 2 = (−2 ± 4i) / 2 = −1 ± 2i.
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 复数的指数形式Exponential form e^(iθ)欧拉公式
这一站要你会：背下欧拉公式 e^(iθ) = cos θ + i sin θ；把复数写成指数形式 z = r e^(iθ)；在极形式与指数形式之间互转。Goal: recall Euler's formula e^(iθ) = cos θ + i sin θ; write z = r e^(iθ); convert between polar and exponential form.
极形式 z = r(cos θ + i sin θ) 写起来有点长，数学家又造了个更短的记号：e^(iθ)。欧拉发现一个惊人的等式：e^(iθ) = cos θ + i sin θ——指数函数和三角函数竟然是一回事。于是复数最简洁的写法是 z = r e^(iθ)：r 管大小，θ 管方向。把 θ 换成 π，得到 e^(iπ) = −1，一条式子把 e、i、π、1、0 五个最重要的数全聚齐了。有了指数形式，乘、除、乘方全变成指数运算，顺手得很。Polar form z = r(cos θ + i sin θ) is a bit long, so mathematicians made a shorter mark: e^(iθ). Euler found the astonishing identity e^(iθ) = cos θ + i sin θ — exponentials and trigonometry are the same thing. The neatest way to write a complex number is z = r e^(iθ): r sets the size, θ the direction. Put θ = π and you get e^(iπ) = −1, gathering the five great constants e, i, π, 1, 0 into one line. With the exponential form, multiply, divide and powers all become plain index rules.
e^(iθ) = cos θ + i sin θ z = r e^(iθ) e^(iπ) = −1
  • 反解三角：cos θ = (e^(iθ) + e^(−iθ)) / 2，sin θ = (e^(iθ) − e^(−iθ)) / 2i。Solve back: cos θ = (e^(iθ) + e^(−iθ)) / 2, sin θ = (e^(iθ) − e^(−iθ)) / 2i.
  • 乘方：zⁿ = rⁿ e^(inθ)，和 de Moivre 完全一致。Powers: zⁿ = rⁿ e^(inθ), exactly matching de Moivre.
符号别混：e^(iθ) 里的 i 在指数上，θ 是辐角。写 e^(iπ) = −1 别漏了 i——没有 i 就是普通指数 e^π，完全是两码事。Watch the symbol: the i in e^(iθ) sits in the exponent and θ is the argument. Don't drop the i in e^(iπ) = −1 — without it you get the ordinary exponential e^π, a totally different thing.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: Euler's formula states that e^(iθ) = cos θ + i sin θ. Hence any complex number can be written z = r e^(iθ), where r = |z| and θ = arg z. In particular e^(iπ) = −1.
例题Example
把 z = 2e^(iπ/3) 写成直角形式 x + iy。Write z = 2e^(iπ/3) in rectangular form x + iy.
① e^(iπ/3) = cos(π/3) + i sin(π/3) = 1/2 + i(√3/2)。① e^(iπ/3) = cos(π/3) + i sin(π/3) = 1/2 + i(√3/2). ② z = 2(1/2 + i√3/2) = 1 + i√3。② z = 2(1/2 + i√3/2) = 1 + i√3.
练习Practice
r = √2、θ = π/4。r = √2, θ = π/4.

把 z = 1 + i 写成指数形式 r e^(iθ)。Write z = 1 + i in exponential form r e^(iθ).

点开看答案Show answer
r = √2、θ = π/4，所以 z = √2 e^(iπ/4)。 r = √2, θ = π/4, so z = √2 e^(iπ/4).
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 几何应用Geometric applications |z − a| = r轨迹=圆
这一站要你会：看懂 |z − a| = r 是圆、|z − a| < r 是圆内；用「乘法 = 旋转 + 缩放」判断乘 i 是转 90°；会描述简单轨迹。Goal: read |z − a| = r as a circle and |z − a| < r as its interior; use "multiplication = rotate + scale" to see that multiplying by i rotates 90°; describe simple loci.
复数不单是代数玩具，还能当几何用，两个视角最值钱：① |z − a| 是距离——z 到 a 的距离，所以 |z − a| = r 是圆，|z − a| < r 是圆内。你以前在坐标几何里背的圆方程 (x−h)²+(y−k)²=r²，复数一句话就写完了。② 乘法 = 旋转 + 缩放——乘一个复数 w，就是把 z 转 arg w 这么个角度、再拉伸 |w| 倍。比如乘 i 就是转 90°（arg i = 90°、|i| = 1）。很多几何变换题，用复数反而更省事。Complex numbers aren't just algebra toys — they do geometry, and two ideas pay off most. ① |z − a| is a distance — the distance from z to a — so |z − a| = r is a circle and |z − a| < r its interior. The circle equation (x−h)²+(y−k)²=r² you memorised collapses to one short line. ② multiplication = rotate + scale — multiplying by w turns z through arg w and stretches it by |w|. Multiply by i and you rotate 90° (arg i = 90°, |i| = 1). Many transformation questions are actually easier in complex form.
|z − a| = r |z − a| < r arg(z − a) = θ
  • |z−a| = r：以 a 为圆心、r 为半径的圆；|z−a| < r 是圆内。|z−a| = r: circle centre a, radius r; |z−a| < r is its interior.
  • arg(z−a) = θ：从 a 出发、方向 θ 的射线。arg(z−a) = θ: a ray from a in direction θ.
  • 乘法：|zw| = |z||w|，arg(zw) = arg z + arg w（旋转 + 缩放）。Multiplication: |zw| = |z||w|, arg(zw) = arg z + arg w (rotate + scale).
乘 i = 转 90°：因为 arg i = π/2、|i| = 1。所以 z、iz、i²z = −z、i³z = −iz 就是绕原点依次转 90°，四个点转一圈。这个直觉在旋转题里很值钱。Multiply by i = rotate 90°: since arg i = π/2 and |i| = 1, the points z, iz, i²z = −z, i³z = −iz step round the origin by 90° each time. This instinct is gold in rotation questions.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: On the Argand diagram, |z − a| is the distance from z to a. Hence |z − a| = r is the circle with centre a and radius r. Multiplying by a complex number w scales by |w| and rotates by arg w.
例题Example
描述方程 |z − (3 + 4i)| = 2 所表示的轨迹，并求圆心和半径。Describe the locus given by |z − (3 + 4i)| = 2, and state its centre and radius.
① 这是「z 到点 3 + 4i 的距离等于 2」的点集。① this is the set of points z whose distance from 3 + 4i is 2. ② 所以是以 3 + 4i 为圆心、2 为半径的圆。② so it is a circle with centre 3 + 4i and radius 2. ③ 圆心 a = 3 + 4i（在 (3, 4)），半径 r = 2。③ centre a = 3 + 4i (at (3, 4)), radius r = 2.
练习Practice
先写成 |z − a| 的样子：a = −1 + 2i。Rewrite as |z − a|: here a = −1 + 2i.

方程 |z + 1 − 2i| = 4 表示什么轨迹？圆心和半径是多少？What locus does |z + 1 − 2i| = 4 describe? Give its centre and radius.

点开看答案Show answer
|z + 1 − 2i| = |z − (−1 + 2i)| = 4。
所以是以 −1 + 2i 为圆心、4 为半径的圆。 |z + 1 − 2i| = |z − (−1 + 2i)| = 4.
So it is the circle with centre −1 + 2i and radius 4.
— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —

单元 12 小结Unit 12 summary

① i² = −1，z = x + iy；模 |z|=√(x²+y²)，辐角 arg z，共轭 z*。
② 三种写法：直角 z=x+iy、极 z=r(cosθ+i sinθ)、指数 z=r e^(iθ)，互转要熟。
③ de Moivre：乘方 = 角度乘 n、模乘 n 次方；zⁿ=1 的 n 个单位根均匀落在单位圆上。
④ 几何：|z−a|=r 是圆；乘 i 转 90°，复数乘法 = 旋转 + 缩放。① i² = −1, z = x + iy; modulus |z|=√(x²+y²), argument arg z, conjugate z*.
② Three forms — rectangular z=x+iy, polar z=r(cosθ+i sinθ), exponential z=r e^(iθ) — convert fluently.
③ de Moivre: powers rotate the angle by n and scale the modulus by rⁿ; the n roots of zⁿ=1 sit evenly on the unit circle.
④ Geometry: |z−a|=r is a circle; multiplying by i rotates 90°, and complex multiplication = rotate + scale.

下一站是本系列最后一单元：微分方程——把「变化率」写成方程，再反解出函数本身。Next, the final unit: differential equations — turning rates of change into equations and solving back for the function.

单元 13 · 微分方程：解出来是「规律」，不是「数」Unit 13 · Differential equations

CAIE 9231 Paper 2 · 5 站 · 方程里藏着导数，解出来是「规律」而不是「数」CAIE 9231 Paper 2 · 5 stops · the equation hides a derivative, and its solution is a "rule", not a "number"

路线图本单元五站：先弄懂微分方程要解的「是一个函数」，再学三种具体解法——分离变量、积分因子、特征方程，最后处理非齐次的「补函数 + 特解」套路Five stops — first see that a differential equation asks for a function, then learn three concrete methods (separating variables, the integrating factor, the characteristic equation), and finally the non-homogeneous "CF + PI" recipe

  1. 微分方程是什么：解 = 函数What a differential equation is
  2. 可分离变量：分两边、各自积分Separable equations
  3. 一阶线性方程：积分因子First-order linear equations
  4. 二阶齐次方程：特征方程Second-order homogeneous
  5. 二阶非齐次：补函数 + 特解Non-homogeneous & particular solutions

建议：微分方程整章就一句话——「导数藏在方程里，把那个满足方程的函数揪出来」。前四站把三种解法练熟，第五站只是「拼装」。Tip: the whole chapter is one idea — a derivative hides in the equation, and you dig out the function that satisfies it. Master the three methods in stops 1–4, and stop 5 is just assembly.

1 微分方程是什么：解是「函数」What a differential equation is Solution = a function解=函数
这一站要你会：说清「微分方程的解是一个函数」，区分通解（含任意常数）与特解（用初值确定）。Goal: state that a solution is a function, and tell a general solution (with an arbitrary constant) from a particular one (fixed by an initial condition).
普通方程解出来是「一个数」——比如 x² = 4，解是 x = ±2。但微分方程里藏着导数 dy/dx，它问的是「哪个函数的导数长这样？」答案自然是一个函数，甚至是一族函数。因为求导会丢掉常数，所以解里会冒出一个任意常数 C，这是「通解」；再告诉你曲线必须过某一点（初值），C 才被定死，得到「特解」。An ordinary equation gives you a number — x² = 4 gives x = ±2. But a differential equation hides a derivative dy/dx, so it asks: which function has this derivative? The answer is naturally a function, in fact a whole family of them. Differentiating drops constants, so the answer carries an arbitrary constant C — that's the general solution; tell it which point the curve must pass through (an initial value), and C gets pinned down to one particular solution.
  • 微分方程：含有导数（dy/dx、d²y/dx²…）的方程。Differential equation: an equation containing derivatives.
  • 解：一个「函数」，代回方程能让两边恒等。Solution: a function that makes both sides equal.
  • 通解：含任意常数 C，表示一族函数。General solution: contains an arbitrary constant C.
  • 特解：用初值（如 y(0)=1）定出 C 后唯一的那条。Particular solution: the unique one after an initial value fixes C.
阶：方程里出现的最高阶导数决定它的「阶」。dy/dx 是一阶，d²y/dx² 是二阶。本单元一、二阶都考，二阶是 Paper 2 的重点。Order: the highest derivative in the equation sets its order — dy/dx is first-order, d²y/dx² second-order. Both are examined; the second-order case is the heart of Paper 2.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The general solution of a differential equation contains one arbitrary constant for a first-order equation (two for a second-order one). A particular solution is obtained by using the initial conditions to determine these constants.
例题Example
验证 y = x² + C 是微分方程 dy/dx = 2x 的通解，并求满足 y(0) = 3 的特解。Show that y = x² + C solves dy/dx = 2x, and find the particular solution with y(0) = 3.
① 对 y = x² + C 求导：dy/dx = 2x，正好等于方程右边，所以它是解（C 任意，是通解）。① differentiate: dy/dx = 2x, which matches the right side, so it is a solution (C arbitrary → general solution). ② 代入初值 y(0)=3：3 = 0² + C → C = 3。② use y(0)=3: 3 = 0² + C → C = 3. ③ 特解：y = x² + 3。③ particular solution: y = x² + 3.
练习Practice
先求导验证，再用初值定 C。Differentiate to verify, then use the initial value to fix C.

验证 y = A·e^(2x) 是 dy/dx = 2y 的解，并求满足 y(0) = 5 的特解。Show that y = A·e^(2x) solves dy/dx = 2y, and find the particular solution with y(0) = 5.

点开看答案Show answer
对 y = A·e^(2x) 求导：dy/dx = 2A·e^(2x) = 2y ✓。
代入 y(0)=5：A·e⁰ = A = 5。特解 y = 5·e^(2x)。 Differentiate: dy/dx = 2A·e^(2x) = 2y ✓.
Use y(0)=5: A = 5. Particular solution y = 5·e^(2x).
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 可分离变量：分两边、各自积分Separable equations Separate & integrate分离再积分
这一站要你会：识别可分离的方程，把含 y 的项挪到 dy 一边、含 x 的挪到 dx 一边，两边积分后整理出通解。Goal: recognise a separable equation, move all the y-terms to the dy side and all the x-terms to the dx side, then integrate both sides to get the general solution.
看到 dy/dx = f(x)g(y)，就像把一袋混在一起的豆子分装：凡是有 y 的（包括 dy）都放到左边，凡是有 x 的（包括 dx）都放到右边。分好之后两边各自积分，就得到了含 C 的通解。关键动作是「分离」——除过去、乘过来，让 dy 只跟 y 作伴、dx 只跟 x 作伴。Seeing dy/dx = f(x)g(y) is like sorting a bag of mixed beans: everything with y (including dy) goes left, everything with x (including dx) goes right. Then integrate each side and out comes the general solution with its C. The key move is the separation — divide and multiply until dy keeps company only with y, and dx only with x.
可分离形式 dy/dx = f(x)g(y) 的三步：Three steps for dy/dx = f(x)g(y):
dy/dx = f(x)·g(y) ⇒ (1/g(y)) dy = f(x) dx ⇒ ∫ (1/g(y)) dy = ∫ f(x) dx
  • 分离：把 g(y) 除到左边、dx 乘到右边 → (1/g(y))dy = f(x)dx。Separate: divide by g(y) and multiply by dx → (1/g(y))dy = f(x)dx.
  • 积分：两边各自积分，任意常数只写一边。Integrate: integrate both sides, writing the constant on one side only.
  • 解出 y：整理出含 C 的通解。Solve for y: rearrange into the general solution with C.
先确认真能拆：分离前看清方程必须能写成 f(x) 乘 g(y)。像 dy/dx = x + y 这种「x、y 搅在一起」的，分离法用不了，得用第 3 站的积分因子。Check it really factors: the right side must split into f(x) times g(y). If x and y are tangled up, as in dy/dx = x + y, separation fails — that's where stop 3's integrating factor steps in.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: If dy/dx = f(x)g(y), we separate the variables to obtain (1/g(y))dy = f(x)dx, then integrate both sides to find the general solution.
例题Example
解微分方程 dy/dx = 2xy。Solve dy/dx = 2xy.
① 分离变量：把 y 除到左边、dx 乘到右边 → (1/y)dy = 2x dx。① separate: divide by y and multiply by dx → (1/y)dy = 2x dx. ② 两边积分：∫(1/y)dy = ∫2x dx → ln|y| = x² + C。② integrate: ∫(1/y)dy = ∫2x dx → ln|y| = x² + C. ③ 两边取指数：|y| = e^(x²+C)，把 e^C 记成新常数 A，得通解 y = A·e^(x²)。③ exponentiate: |y| = e^(x²+C); absorb e^C into a new constant A → y = A·e^(x²).
练习Practice
分离后再积分，别忘了常数 C。Separate first, then integrate — don't forget C.

解微分方程 dy/dx = y/x（x > 0）。Solve dy/dx = y/x (x > 0).

点开看答案Show answer
分离：(1/y)dy = (1/x)dx。
积分：ln y = ln x + C。
取指数：y = e^C·x = A·x（A 为任意常数）。 Separate: (1/y)dy = (1/x)dx.
Integrate: ln y = ln x + C.
Exponentiate: y = e^C·x = A·x (A arbitrary).
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 一阶线性方程：积分因子First-order linear equations Integrating factor积分因子
这一站要你会：把方程写成标准形 dy/dx + P(x)y = Q(x)，求出积分因子 μ = e^(∫P dx)，乘上后左边变成 (μy)′，积分解出 y。Goal: write the equation as dy/dx + P(x)y = Q(x), find μ = e^(∫P dx), multiply through so the left side becomes (μy)′, then integrate to solve for y.
分离变量怕的是「x、y 搅在一起」，而一阶线性方程 dy/dx + P(x)y = Q(x) 正是这种搅在一起的典型。破解办法是一根「魔法因子」：μ = e^(∫P dx)。把它乘到两边，左边会神奇地变成 (μy) 的导数——就像给一团乱麻找到了「逆运算」的钥匙，之后一积分就散开了。Separating variables stumbles when x and y are tangled, and the first-order linear equation dy/dx + P(x)y = Q(x) is exactly that tangle. The fix is a magic factor: μ = e^(∫P dx). Multiply both sides by it and the left side collapses into the derivative of μy — like finding the key to an "inverse operation", after which one integration untangles everything.
一阶线性 dy/dx + P(x)y = Q(x) 的四步：Four steps for dy/dx + P(x)y = Q(x):
μ = e^(∫ P(x) dx) d/dx (μ·y) = μ·Q(x) μ·y = ∫ μ·Q(x) dx + C
  • 标准形：把 y′ 系数变成 1，认出 P(x)、Q(x)。Standard form: make the coefficient of y′ equal to 1 and identify P(x), Q(x).
  • 求 μ：μ = e^(∫P dx)，积分时不用管那个常数（会约掉）。Find μ: μ = e^(∫P dx); ignore the constant here (it cancels out).
  • 乘 μ 合并：左边变成 d/dx(μy) = μQ(x)。Multiply and merge: left becomes d/dx(μy) = μQ(x).
  • 积分解 y：μy = ∫μQ dx + C，再除以 μ。Integrate for y: μy = ∫μQ dx + C, then divide by μ.
为什么乘 μ 有用：μ 的选法保证 μ′ = μP，于是 (μy)′ = μy′ + μPy = μ(y′ + Py) = μQ。记住「乘 μ 后左边必能合并成 (μy)′」就够了。Why μ works: μ is chosen so μ′ = μP, hence (μy)′ = μy′ + μPy = μ(y′ + Py) = μQ. Just remember "after multiplying by μ, the left side must merge into (μy)′".
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: To solve dy/dx + P(x)y = Q(x), multiply through by the integrating factor μ = e^(∫P dx). The left-hand side becomes d/dx(μy), so integrating gives μy = ∫ μQ dx.
例题Example
解微分方程 dy/dx + 2y = 4x。Solve dy/dx + 2y = 4x.
① 已是标准形 dy/dx + 2y = 4x，这里 P(x) = 2、Q(x) = 4x。① already standard: P(x) = 2, Q(x) = 4x. ② 积分因子：μ = e^(∫2 dx) = e^(2x)。② integrating factor μ = e^(2x). ③ 乘 μ：(e^(2x)·y)′ = 4x·e^(2x)。③ multiply: (e^(2x)·y)′ = 4x·e^(2x). ④ 积分：e^(2x)y = 2x·e^(2x) − e^(2x) + C，两边除以 e^(2x) → y = 2x − 1 + C·e^(−2x)。④ integrate: e^(2x)y = 2x·e^(2x) − e^(2x) + C; divide by e^(2x) → y = 2x − 1 + C·e^(−2x).
练习Practice
先认出 P(x) 求 μ，右边的 ∫μQ dx 可能要分部积分。Identify P(x) to find μ; the integral ∫μQ dx may need integration by parts.

解微分方程 dy/dx + y = x。Solve dy/dx + y = x.

点开看答案Show answer
P(x) = 1 → μ = e^x。
乘 μ：(e^x·y)′ = x·e^x。积分：e^x·y = x·e^x − e^x + C。
通解 y = x − 1 + C·e^(−x)。 P(x) = 1 → μ = e^x.
Multiply: (e^x·y)′ = x·e^x. Integrate: e^x·y = x·e^x − e^x + C.
General solution y = x − 1 + C·e^(−x).
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 二阶齐次方程：特征方程Second-order homogeneous equations Characteristic equation特征方程
这一站要你会：写出特征方程 am² + bm + c = 0，求出它的根，并按「两实根 / 重根 / 复根」三种情况写出通解。Goal: write the characteristic equation am² + bm + c = 0, find its roots, and give the general solution in each of the three cases — distinct, repeated or complex.
二阶齐次方程 ay″ + by′ + cy = 0，长得像「加速度 + 速度 + 位置 = 0」。猜它解的形式是 e^(mx)——代进去你会发现，e^(mx) 永远提不没，剩下一个关于 m 的二次方程 am² + bm + c = 0，这就是「特征方程」。m 的根有几种，通解就有几种样子，全看那个二次方程的判别式 Δ = b² − 4ac。The homogeneous second-order equation ay″ + by′ + cy = 0 looks like "acceleration + velocity + position = 0". Guess a solution of the form e^(mx) — substitute and you'll find e^(mx) never vanishes, leaving a quadratic am² + bm + c = 0 in m: the characteristic equation. How many forms the solution takes depends on its discriminant Δ = b² − 4ac.
特征方程 am² + bm + c = 0 的三种情况：The three cases of am² + bm + c = 0:
两实根 m₁ ≠ m₂：y = A·e^(m₁x) + B·e^(m₂x) 重根 m：y = (A + Bx)·e^(mx) 复根 α ± iβ：y = e^(αx)·(A·cos βx + B·sin βx)
  • 判别式定方向：Δ = b² − 4ac，大于 0 两实根、等于 0 重根、小于 0 复根。The discriminant decides: Δ = b² − 4ac — positive gives two real roots, zero a repeated root, negative complex roots.
  • 复根取实虚部：m = α ± iβ 时，α 进指数、β 进三角。Complex roots split: for m = α ± iβ, α goes into the exponential and β into the trig part.
重根最容易被漏：只有一个 m 时，通解不是 A e^(mx)（那会少一个任意常数），而是 (A + Bx)e^(mx)——多一个 x。复根则记住「实部管指数、虚部管三角」：e^(αx)(A cos βx + B sin βx)。The repeated root is the classic miss: with a single m, the general solution is not A e^(mx) (that would drop a constant) but (A + Bx)e^(mx) — one extra x. For complex roots, remember "the real part drives the exponential, the imaginary part drives the trig".
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: For ay″ + by′ + cy = 0 we solve the auxiliary equation am² + bm + c = 0. Distinct real roots m₁, m₂ give y = A e^(m₁x) + B e^(m₂x); a repeated root m gives y = (A + Bx)e^(mx); complex roots α ± iβ give y = e^(αx)(A cos βx + B sin βx).
例题Example
解二阶微分方程 y″ − 5y′ + 6y = 0。Solve y″ − 5y′ + 6y = 0.
① 特征方程：m² − 5m + 6 = 0。① characteristic equation: m² − 5m + 6 = 0. ② 因式分解 (m−2)(m−3) = 0 → 两不相等实根 m = 2、3。② factorise (m−2)(m−3) = 0 → distinct real roots m = 2, 3. ③ 通解：y = A·e^(2x) + B·e^(3x)。③ general solution: y = A·e^(2x) + B·e^(3x).
练习Practice
先写特征方程，看判别式定情况。Write the characteristic equation first, then let the discriminant decide.

解微分方程 y″ + y′ − 2y = 0。Solve y″ + y′ − 2y = 0.

点开看答案Show answer
特征方程 m² + m − 2 = 0 → (m+2)(m−1) = 0 → m = −2、1（两不相等实根）。
通解 y = A·e^(−2x) + B·e^(x)。 Characteristic equation m² + m − 2 = 0 → (m+2)(m−1) = 0 → m = −2, 1 (two distinct real roots).
General solution y = A·e^(−2x) + B·e^(x).
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 二阶非齐次：补函数 + 特解Non-homogeneous & particular solutions CF + PI补函数+特解
这一站要你会：把非齐次通解拆成「补函数 CF + 特解 PI」，会按 f(x) 的形状猜 PI 的形式（多项式、指数、三角），代入定系数。Goal: split the general solution into CF + PI, guess the form of PI from the shape of f(x) (polynomial, exponential, trig), and substitute to find its coefficients.
非齐次方程右边多了个 f(x)，别慌，套路是「一半管自己，一半管右边」。先当 f(x)=0 解出补函数 CF（这就是第 4 站干的活）；再照着 f(x) 的长相猜一个特解 PI——f(x) 是多项式就猜多项式，是指数就猜指数，是三角就猜三角——代回方程把系数定出来；最后加起来就完事。The non-homogeneous equation carries an extra f(x) on the right — don't panic, the recipe is "half handles itself, half handles the right side". First set f(x)=0 and solve the complementary function CF (exactly stop 4's job); then guess a particular integral PI matching the shape of f(x) — polynomial for a polynomial, exponential for an exponential, trig for trig — substitute to pin the coefficients; finally add them up and you're done.
通解 = CF + PI 的套路：The CF + PI recipe:
y = y_CF + y_PI
  • CF（补函数）：解齐次 ay″ + by′ + cy = 0 的通解（第 4 站）。CF (complementary function): the general solution of the homogeneous equation ay″ + by′ + cy = 0 (stop 4).
  • PI（特解）：按 f(x) 形式猜，代入定系数。PI (particular integral): guess its form from f(x), then find the coefficients by substitution.
  • 猜形对照：f(x) 是多项式 → 猜同次多项式；f(x) = k·e^(λx) → 猜 A·e^(λx)；f(x) = k·cos ωx 或 k·sin ωx → 猜 A·cos ωx + B·sin ωx。Guess table: polynomial f(x) → a polynomial of the same degree; f(x) = k·e^(λx) → A·e^(λx); f(x) = k·cos ωx or k·sin ωx → A·cos ωx + B·sin ωx.
撞车要乘 x：如果 PI 猜的形式和 CF 里已经有的项「撞车」，要给它乘个 x（重根再乘 x²），否则代入会消掉、定不出系数。Multiply by x on a clash: if the guessed PI collides with a term already inside the CF, multiply it by x (by x² for a repeated root) — otherwise substitution cancels it out and the coefficients can't be found.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：How to say it in the exam: The general solution of the non-homogeneous equation ay″ + by′ + cy = f(x) is y = CF + PI, where CF is the general solution of the homogeneous equation and PI is any particular integral. The form of PI is guessed from f(x) and its coefficients found by substitution.
例题Example
解 y″ − 5y′ + 6y = 12。Solve y″ − 5y′ + 6y = 12.
① 补函数 CF：解 y″−5y′+6y = 0 → 特征方程 m²−5m+6 = 0 → m = 2、3 → CF = A·e^(2x) + B·e^(3x)。① CF from y″−5y′+6y = 0: m²−5m+6 = 0 → m = 2, 3 → CF = A·e^(2x) + B·e^(3x). ② 右边 f(x) = 12 是常数，猜特解 PI = k（常数）。代入：0 − 0 + 6k = 12 → k = 2。② f(x) = 12 is constant, so try PI = k: 0 − 0 + 6k = 12 → k = 2. ③ 通解：y = A·e^(2x) + B·e^(3x) + 2。③ general solution: y = A·e^(2x) + B·e^(3x) + 2.
练习Practice
先解齐次得 CF，再按 f(x) 猜 PI。Solve the homogeneous equation for CF, then guess PI from f(x).

解 y″ + y′ − 2y = 4。Solve y″ + y′ − 2y = 4.

点开看答案Show answer
CF：解 y″+y′−2y = 0 → m = −2、1 → A·e^(−2x) + B·e^(x)。
f(x) = 4 是常数，猜 PI = k：−2k = 4 → k = −2。
通解 y = A·e^(−2x) + B·e^(x) − 2。 CF: solve y″+y′−2y = 0 → m = −2, 1 → A·e^(−2x) + B·e^(x).
f(x) = 4 is constant, try PI = k: −2k = 4 → k = −2.
General solution y = A·e^(−2x) + B·e^(x) − 2.
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —

单元 13 小结Unit 13 summary

① 微分方程的解是「函数」，通解含任意常数，特解用初值确定。
② 三种解法：可分离变量（分两边各自积分）；一阶线性（积分因子 μ = e^(∫P dx)）；二阶齐次（特征方程，两实根 / 重根 / 复根三种通解）。
③ 非齐次：通解 = 补函数 CF + 特解 PI，PI 按 f(x) 的样子猜、代入定系数。① a solution is a function; the general solution carries an arbitrary constant, the particular one is fixed by initial values.
② three methods: separable (split and integrate), first-order linear (integrating factor μ = e^(∫P dx)), second-order homogeneous (characteristic equation → distinct / repeated / complex roots).
③ non-homogeneous: y = CF + PI, with PI guessed from f(x) and found by substitution.

到这里，13 个单元全部完成。微分方程是进阶纯数 2 的最后一站，也是「把微积分用起来」的收尾——先弄懂「解是函数」，再把三种解法练成肌肉记忆，祝你在考场上稳稳拿下。With this, all 13 units are complete. Differential equations close Further Pure Mathematics 2 — and put calculus to work. Nail the idea that "the solution is a function", drill the three methods until they're automatic, and good luck in the exam.

进阶数学公式多，但都是「工具型」——记住「公式 + 它解决什么问题」成对背 · 点上面的单元 tab 随时回去看讲解Further Maths has many formulas, but they are all tools — learn each formula together with the problem it solves · jump back to any unit tab

按主题分组grouped by theme

  1. 根与系数Roots and coefficients
  2. 级数求和Summation of series
  3. 矩阵Matrices
  4. 极坐标与向量Polar and vectors
  5. 双曲函数Hyperbolic functions
  6. 复数Complex numbers
  7. 微分方程Differential equations
  8. 归纳法模板Induction template

根与系数Roots and coefficients

二次 ax²+bx+c=0（根 α,β）：α+β = −b/a，αβ = c/a 三次 ax³+bx²+cx+d=0（根 α,β,γ）： α+β+γ = −b/a；αβ+βγ+γα = c/a；αβγ = −d/a 四次 ax⁴+…+e=0（根 α,β,γ,δ）： α+β+γ+δ = −b/a；两两和 = c/a；三三和 = −d/a；αβγδ = e/a 造方程：x² − (和)x + (积) = 0 对称式：α²+β²+γ² = (α+β+γ)² − 2(αβ+βγ+γα)

级数求和Summation of series

Σr = n(n+1)/2 Σr² = n(n+1)(2n+1)/6 Σr³ = [n(n+1)/2]² 差分法：把通项写成 f(r) − f(r+1)，首尾相消 例 1/(r(r+1)) = 1/r − 1/(r+1)

矩阵Matrices

2×2 行列式 det[a b; c d] = ad − bc 逆矩阵 A⁻¹ = (1/det)[d −b; −c a]（det≠0） 解方程组：AX=B → X = A⁻¹B 特征值：解 det(A − λI) = 0 特征向量：代入 λ 解 (A − λI)v = 0 对角化：A = PDP⁻¹ → Aⁿ = PDⁿP⁻¹ 旋转矩阵 [cosθ −sinθ; sinθ cosθ]

极坐标与向量Polar and vectors

直角↔极坐标：x = r cosθ，y = r sinθ，r² = x²+y² 极坐标面积：A = ½ ∫ r² dθ 点积：a·b = |a||b| cosθ（垂直 ⟺ a·b = 0） 叉积：a×b 垂直于 a、b；|a×b| = |a||b| sinθ 直线：r = a + tb 平面：r·n = d

双曲函数Hyperbolic functions

sinh x = (eˣ − e⁻ˣ)/2，cosh x = (eˣ + e⁻ˣ)/2，tanh x = sinh/cosh 恒等式：cosh²x − sinh²x = 1 反函数：arsinh x = ln(x + √(x²+1)) 导数：d(sinh x)/dx = cosh x，d(cosh x)/dx = sinh x

复数Complex numbers

z = x + iy，i² = −1；|z| = √(x²+y²) 极形式：z = r(cosθ + i sinθ) de Moivre：(cosθ + i sinθ)ⁿ = cos(nθ) + i sin(nθ) 欧拉公式：e^(iθ) = cosθ + i sinθ zⁿ = 1 有 n 个根，均匀分布在单位圆上

微分方程Differential equations

分离变量：dy/dx = f(x)g(y) → ∫(1/g)dy = ∫f dx 一阶线性：dy/dx + P y = Q，积分因子 e^∫P dx 二阶齐次 ay''+by'+cy=0：特征方程 am²+bm+c=0 实根 r₁,r₂ → y = Ae^(r₁x)+Be^(r₂x) 重根 r → y = (A+Bx)e^(rx) 复根 p±qi → y = e^(px)(A cos qx + B sin qx) 非齐次：通解 = 补函数 + 特解

归纳法模板Induction template

① 基础情形：验证 n = 1（或最小 n）成立 ② 归纳假设：假设 n = k 时命题成立 ③ 归纳步：证明 n = k+1 也成立 ④ 结论：由归纳法，对所有 n ≥ 1 成立

速查表到此结束End of the cheat sheet

临考三步：① 默写根与系数 + 级数求和 → ② 过一遍双曲/复数的定义与恒等式 → ③ 背熟归纳法四步与二阶微分方程的三种情况。进阶数学拼的是熟练度，公式要闭眼能写。Three steps: ① write the root–coefficient and summation formulas → ② run through the hyperbolic/complex definitions and identities → ③ learn the induction template and the three second-order cases. Further Maths runs on fluency — formulas must come out with your eyes shut.

三、没有电脑也能练的 8 件事3 · Eight things to practise without a computer

  1. 默写根与系数的二次/三次/四次三组公式。Write the root–coefficient formulas for quadratic, cubic and quartic.
  2. 默写 Σr、Σr²、Σr³ 三个标准结果。Recall the three standard series sums.
  3. 算一个 2×2 矩阵的行列式和逆矩阵。Compute the determinant and inverse of a 2×2 matrix.
  4. 把 z = 1+i 写成极形式，并算 z⁴。Write z = 1+i in polar form and compute z⁴.
  5. 默写 sinh、cosh 的定义和 cosh²−sinh²=1。Recall the definitions of sinh, cosh and cosh²−sinh²=1.
  6. 用归纳法完整证明 Σr = n(n+1)/2。Prove Σr = n(n+1)/2 by induction, in full.
  7. 解 y'' − 5y' + 6y = 0（写特征方程和通解）。Solve y'' − 5y' + 6y = 0 (characteristic equation and general solution).
  8. 用差分法算 Σ 1/(r(r+1)) 从 1 到 n。Use the method of differences on Σ 1/(r(r+1)) from 1 to n.

资源就这些，够用了That is all you need

进阶数学不靠海量资料——看明白本页 13 个单元 + 每天练熟一两个技巧，比收藏一堆网站有用得多。重点看 3Blue1Brown 的线性代数系列，那几集顶十页讲义。Further Maths doesn't need a mountain of resources — understand the 13 units here and drill a couple of techniques a day. Above all, watch 3Blue1Brown's linear algebra series — a few episodes are worth ten pages of notes.

先懂原理，再记定理，最后才是熟练度Understand the idea, learn the theorem, then drill for fluency

进阶数学一半是新工具（矩阵、复数、极坐标、双曲函数），一半是新思维（归纳法、差分法、特征值）。配图和「说人话」帮你搞懂每个工具是干嘛的；🗣 考场英文怎么说 帮你把证明题的分拿到；例题负责演示怎么用。别死记硬背——每个技巧都先问一句「它解决什么问题」，想通了才记得牢。Further Maths is half new tools (matrices, complex numbers, polar coordinates, hyperbolic functions) and half new thinking (induction, method of differences, eigenvalues). The diagrams and "in plain words" boxes show what each tool is for; the 🗣 How to say it in the exam boxes win the proof marks; the examples show how to use them. Don't memorise blindly — ask what problem each technique solves; once that clicks it sticks.
